### The
## outer and inner
# Golden
# Eye-Mirror :
<note>original: "Augen-Spiegel". In the 17th and 18th centuries, a "mirror" or "spectacles" was a common metaphor for a book that provides spiritual insight or a way to see the truth more clearly.</note>
### Forged and written
### from Him /
## Who is able to make all things
## come alive.
<note>This refers to God as the source of life and inspiration for the text.</note>
### Presents
# a threefold
# God-man and Beast-man
<note>This reflects the mystical concept that humanity contains both a divine nature (God-man) and a corrupted, earthly nature (Beast-man).</note>
## before and after the Fall
<note>The "Fall" refers to the biblical <term>Fall of Man</term> in the Garden of Eden.</note>
## of Adam.
### Published by a lover of the truth.
<note>original: "Liebhaber der Warheit". This was a frequent pseudonym for authors of alchemical, theosophical, or mystical texts who wished to remain anonymous while signaling their devotion to spiritual wisdom.</note>

## In the year of Christ 1713.

## Explanation
# of the Engraved Plate.

The <term>Engraved Plate<note>original: "Kupffer-Blat"; a copperplate engraving used to print detailed illustrations.</note></term> represents the four primary worlds—before, during, and after Creation—with their forty-eight gates surrounding them. How each individual star-world, star, gate, number, and other elements contained within are named, and what significance each holds, is explained herein and indicated by the large and small Latin alphabet.

->A<-

This is the entirely corrupt heart, the carnal world of the dead, where everything is dead and dark, where no day nor even the slightest light reaches. It stands in the <note>This imagery draws on Revelation 11:8, where "Sodom and Egypt" symbolize spiritual blindness and the oppression of the soul.</note> figure of Sodom and Egypt, where our LORD was crucified. However, it also specifically refers to the entirely corrupt, unrefined, carnal person who exists in complete harmony and likeness with the lowest abyss, where the Devil fell at the beginning after his apostasy from God. For all <term>worldly people<note>original: "Welt-Kinder"; literally "world-children," a term for those preoccupied with material life rather than spiritual growth.</note></term> are the <note>The "Devil's ape" (simia Diaboli) is a traditional concept suggesting that evil has no creativity of its own and can only mimic or distort God's work.</note> Devil's apes, who mimic and follow him in everything. They flee the Cross of Christ and trample the Word of God—the light of the world, the truth, and the life—underfoot, pushing it away from themselves. They refuse to hear God in His holy order, crucifying the light and obstructing it so that it cannot shine upon them, even though it would gladly shine. Consequently, they have become like him in all things; and in the end, they will also share his

Punishments must be partaken of in the lower, dark abyss.

Here there is no light, but darkness is the sun and the moon, which never shine and have only dark, fiery bodies. There is a star as dark as soot and coals, and yet they are full of fire, but <term>living-dead<note>original: "lebendig-todt"; a common mystical paradox describing a state of spiritual death where the soul functions physically but is severed from divine life.</note></term>. Thus, the **Lamb** also does not dwell here in the heart with its light, but must remain outside, hated by all, driven away, blasphemed, and killed. For in the perverted heart, Christ is crucified and dead, and does not live within it. But the **Dragon** and the **Beast** dwell there, with snakes and all dark devilish phantoms, which have shackled and bound the **heart** behind its back with their **chains of power**. And to these—namely the dragon, the beast, and all devils—this door of the heart stands always open through their life of vice; but the Lamb is not let in, however firmly it stands before the door and knocks. Thus, here truly is the <note>original: "verkehrte Welt"; literally "perverted" or "overturned world," referring to a state where the natural and divine order is flipped.</note> upside-down world.

The star with the twelve horns is the false prophet of the dark world with the twelve dark births. Just as in the Kingdom of Light the prophet **Aaron has twelve precious stones** <note>A reference to the "Breastplate of Judgment" (Exodus 28:15–21) worn by the High Priest, containing twelve stones representing the tribes of Israel.</note> in light and right upon his heart, so the false prophet has the coat of arms of the devil and his son upon his heart, namely darkness and injustice; and that is the seed with which he is impregnated by the devil. Their children, however, are the <note>A reference to the 144,000 mentioned in the Book of Revelation, here adapted into a cosmological "star-circle" geometry.</note> one hundred and forty-four in the star-circles.

The great **star** with the bright **radiance**, **seven horns**, **seven eyes**, and **seven torches**, is **Christ**, the slaughtered **Lamb** <note>This imagery is drawn directly from Revelation 5:6, representing the perfect power and omniscience of Christ.</note>, the Word of Creation of the Father, which in the beginning of the Crea-

> I will redeem them from the graves / and rescue them from death; Death / I will be a poison to you! Hell / I will be a pestilence to you! <note>A translation of Hosea 13:14, often cited in early modern theology to describe the Harrowing of Hell.</note>

# Explanation

**i** In the ninth <note>gate</note>, the Light battles with the terrifying Darkness and suffers much resistance from the damned.

**k** In the tenth, Christ, the Prince of Light, is crucified.

**l** In the eleventh, Christ lies in death and is buried.

**m** In the twelfth, he rises and breaks through the kingdom of Hell and of Death, and leads the soul across with him into the God-ordained Kingdom of Canaan <note>Canaan is used here as a symbol for the promised spiritual rest or the state of grace</note>. There, he begins to purify and regenerate the soul in the barren wilderness of the world; just as a prototype can be seen in Israel, which departed from Egypt into the wilderness—which is now the half-light and half-dark world itself, and which it also contains within.

Between the twelfth and the first gates, the dark Dragon of the Heart has a long tail extending up through the perverse heart; and thus the fleshly Jewish world <note>The author likely uses "Jewish world" here as a mystical trope for those bound to the letter of the law or material ritual rather than the spirit</note> is shackled and pulled from below by the heathen world. The tail, however, is the false power and strength of the Beast and the Dragon, which he possesses in both worlds: the dark and the half-dark, where darkness and light are mixed together. For even this half-light world lies in wickedness following the Fall.

Inside the twelve gates, the fleshly birth is now indicated, which is born from the 144 <term>Star-Spirits</term> <note>Astrological or angelic forces believed to influence human nature</note> and princes of the Dragon.

**n** The beginning of the dark birth is ten.

**o** The growth in the second Star-Gate is two hundred.

**p** The third age in the growth of evil is three thousand.

<margin>B</margin>

of fugitive men and angels of the abyss <note>original: "Abarunds," an archaic variant of Abgrund, referring to the bottomless pit or hell</note>. And thus, the only difference between the great world <note>the macrocosm or universe</note> and the small world <note>the microcosm or individual human</note> is this: that what the small Adam of this world does with so many thousands of powers, the great World-Adam <note>the universal human or cosmic being</note> does with so many thousands of angels and men. For a king has greater power and authority than a beggar. Therefore, his sin is also more and heavier than a beggar's sin. For the beggar's capacity to practice sin is not as great as the king's capacity. Therefore, the king's sins are very great and heavy because of his power. And so also the sins in a lowly person, though they be great, are yet not so great as the sins of the whole world. On the other hand, however, the lowly person still has a birth and sin within himself from all the sins of the whole world and from all its offspring, which will then be just as damnable to him as the great and heavy sin of the great world will be to it, which will plunge it into damnation.

# of the Copperplate.

The world presented here is Israel according to the flesh <note>original: "Israel nach dem Fleisch"; a Pauline concept referring to the physical lineage or legalistic practice of Judaism as opposed to the spiritual "Israel."</note> / the God-legalistic Jew / the half-light human in the God-legalistic present world / the legalistic Outer Court / where the Slaughter-Altar of GOD stands with its sacrifices.

The two uppermost Cross-Heights and the expanse with the six circles are attributed to the Day and Light; however, the two lowermost Cross-Depths and the breadth / with the six circles, Angel-Numbers, and stars / are attributed to the Darkness.

Look upon the sketch of this Heart-World lying half in death!

On the left hand, it is entirely night and dark within the heart. This is the part in Adam that has fallen away from GOD; it clings to the dark abyss. Here it is twelve hours of night; that is: The twelve births of the sinful human are darkness and gloom on the dark left side / where the false prophet stands with the twelve false births of power and strength; alongside which is also the Beast with the ten royal Law-Horns and crowns of the seductive course of the world and the seven spirits of error; with the fiery red dragon / who even enters into the holy-legalistic Paradise-Light-World / and fights with the Woman of the Sun / namely with the God-legalistic soul / who puts off the stained dark garment of sin / and puts on white holy Light-Garments / which are righteousnesses and all kinds of veracity and truths in sanctification.

The half and full moon is in the darkness of the heart / which is surrounded and filled with many serpents and night-specters.

# Explanation

The Light-Side to the right hand of the Heart is the God-ordained World of Light, wherein the Word is Sun and Light, and where the seven golden candlesticks—representing the seven angelic congregations of God—dwell. Among these, the seven Star-Angels are princes; namely, the seven angels of the Temple clothed in pure, bright linen, acting as seven holy planetary stars who teach the Law of God within the great Temple of the seven great congregations of Jesus Christ.

<margin>a</margin> The Center of the Heart is the God-ordained Sun of Nature; it signifies Jesus, the Sun of the World, who was crucified not only for our sins, but also for the sins of the entire world.

<margin>b</margin> The Sword of His Mouth is the Word of God, which splits the darkness asunder like a fiery sword of the spiritual Law. It penetrates all things and gives light to the Moon, separating the darkness and gloom from around it.

<margin>c</margin> The seven Star-Angels.

<margin>d</margin> The seven candlesticks.

<margin>e</margin> The Woman’s crown of twelve stars. <note>A reference to the "Woman clothed with the Sun" from Revelation 12.</note> The twelve stars are the twelve day-births in the Light, the twelve Elders of the souls, belonging to the God-ordained Temple and Jerusalem. For wherever a God-ordained Temple and a Jerusalem exist, day and night are always equal; thus, the day there has twelve hours, as does the night. Whoever walks in the day does not stumble. # Description of the Copperplate Engraving

...the soul falls into a deep midnight, where the night and darkness continually increase, when the days of the law-abiding soul begin to grow short and she sinks into sleep. The further into midnight, the shorter the day. In some places eight hours; in some six; in some places no more than two hours. And then comes the judgment, and the Prince of Darkness is punished by the light when it breaks forth early in the morning. Winter is a long night that devours almost the entire summer day and swallows all the good things that the summer brought forth in the morning, when the twenty-four Elders of Light sit in government, who, like great kings, give birth to their children in great multitudes. --- Page 20 ---
<meta>This page continues the mystical explanation of the celestial struggle between the "Woman" (representing the soul or the Church) and the "Dragon" (representing darkness), focusing on the transition from the previous page's description of spiritual warfare to the specific structure of the celestial gates.</meta>

# Explanation

Temptations <note>original: "Anfechtungen" — refers to spiritual trials or assaults by the devil</note> which he [the dragon] spews out of his mouth after her. From above, the paradisiacal light descends as a strong power of Christ for the comfort of the Woman; and from below, the Dragon’s tail and the power of darkness rise for the torment of the Woman.

---

The twelve circular zeros <note>likely referring to a diagram or illustration of twelve circles or spheres</note> are the twelve gates of this starry world within the four great depths. Of these, the light-side has six gates with 72 stars, and the dark part has just as many. Thus, each part contains only half of the common stars, as indicated by the numerical sums, until the Lamb triumphs and overcomes the dark part through the sword of His mouth.

The light-side possesses the half-part, and thus its six gates of light.

**A** In the first gate, the soul in its initial birth is harshly persecuted by the Dragon beneath the paradise of God's Law.

**B** The second gate, with its twelve source-spirits or star-princes, borders upon the holy paradise of *Jesus Christ*.

**C** In the third starry gate, the source-princes of God's Law and the upper paradisiacal star- or source-princes work through one another, descending and ascending into this world and the next.

**D** In the fourth gate, it is shown how we must receive our light from the pure world of Paradise and its light-constellation.

**E. F** In the fifth and sixth gates, the end of the half-light world is shown.

In these six gates of light, there are exactly six times twelve, or seventy-two, source-spirits of the stars.

# Explanation

Inside, on the dark side, there now follows the fleshly offspring of sins, which the fleshly-minded **Israel** <note>In this mystical context, "Israel" refers to the soul or the people of God when they are turned toward worldly or "fleshly" desires rather than spiritual ones.</note> brings forth within the soul. This is a perverted seed; therefore, the numbers and the characters of the letters are, not without reason, placed in reverse.

Z Firstly, the dark **Serpent** brings forth her first image in the gateway above the **Beast**, with a **Zero**, which is ten.

Y In the second gateway, she brings forth two hundred.

X In the third, she brings forth three thousand.

W In the fourth, she brings forth forty thousand.

V In the fifth gateway, she brings forth five hundred thousand.

T And in the sixth gateway, the seventy-two-fold starry **Serpent** brings forth the six-million-fold dark birth of sin from the flesh.

->C<-

This is the truly Christian **Cross-Heart** and pure **Paradise-World**, the city and dwelling of the right-believing soul after the death and passing away of all sins; and also, at the same time, the awakened holy heart from within—the sanctified, pure **Paradise-** and **Overcoming-Soul**, which has triumphed under the Cross and has been crowned with the most precious and highly-valued **Paradise-Crown**, consisting of 24 **Stars** all around. These are the true births of life, born out of the light of the essence of **God**. These worship **God** in the sanctified heart-soul by casting down their crowns, and they bring forth fruit to their **God** with gratitude.

# Of the Copperplate.

The middlemost Heart in this world is the golden, holy Paradise of Jesus Christ.

The large Cross with its four parts signifies the height, depth, length, and breadth of Paradise. <note>A reference to Ephesians 3:18, describing the dimensions of divine love.</note>

The Sun in the center of the Heart is Christ, the beautiful Light of Paradise—the eternal Word of Creation—which wells up like a great stream in holy souls and makes the entire soul light in its four regions.

**A** The twenty-four stars in the Light-Cross are the twenty-four Elders who worship before the Lamb of God <note>original: "Lamm GOttes"</note> and bring forth one praise of God after another every hour.

**B** The 24 Crown- and Throne-Elders, as they go forth from the new Heart, constitute the holy Crown.

**C** The four stars around the Sun-light are the four great Sabbaths in the months—the rest in Christ. This fourfold monthly Cherubic Sabbath of God is the fourfold sanctified Cherubic soul, as soon as the Word of Creation has risen within it as a light that illuminates it; or it is the Word of Life itself, which goes forth from the Lamb as four Cherubim, and in which the Cherubic soul rests. When these four Cherubim of the Word enter into the soul, the first is like a **Lion**; that is the masculine zeal against sin and all impurities. The second is like an **Ox**; that is the discipline of the Law, which is tamed through sin when a person willingly takes the gentle yoke of Christ upon themselves. The third has a **Face like a Man**; that is the sanctification of the Cross, through which the soul is sanctified

# Preface.

Beloved souls! This entire little book deals with nothing other than the threefold evil and good life that is within man: of **God**, that is, of the Father, Son, and Spirit, in a single essence of the Trinity <note>original: "Dreyheit"</note> of **God**; and of the Anti-**God**, that is, of the threefold dark nature and the sectarian or dragon-spirit of this world's course, which has its seat and dwelling in the animalistic man and the crafty serpent-man on the one side; just as **God** on the other side desires to have and creates His dwelling within man with His entire triune Godhead. And this is the content of the entire little book throughout: that it discovers the internal state of man and what is actually within man.

I did not, indeed, originally write it with the intention that I would allow it to go out into general print: but rather

<summary>The author introduces the book's central theme: the internal struggle between the divine Trinity and the "dragon-spirit" within the human soul. He explains that the work serves to reveal the true internal state of man's spiritual nature.</summary>
<keywords>Trinity, spiritual warfare, internal state, dragon-spirit, serpent-man, triune Godhead, human nature</keywords>

more for this reason: because for a long time now, God has clearly shown and placed before my eyes my internal corruption—and thus the corruption of all men—as if in a mirror. In this <term>recognition</term><note>German: Erkäntnuß. In this context, it refers to a deep, experiential spiritual realization rather than mere intellectual knowledge.</note> of my own and all men's ruin, He gave me the freedom to commit one thing after another to paper whenever the spring was open to look into with the inner eye of the heart's <term>disposition</term><note>German: Gemüth. A central term in German Pietism referring to the seat of emotions, will, and spirit.</note>. I did this so that it might be preserved for me as a treasure, and that I might let it serve me during other parched times, should the spring of self-knowledge run dry and I be forced to taste and feel once more the drought of my own wilderness—which is best known to my God, and is no longer hidden from me either. Lord, strike hard, and do not spare! Otherwise, my own spirit might devour and swallow me up.

Ah, what a great corruption have we inherited from the Devil and the world! What depths of the abyss has the angel of destruction grafted and planted into me and all the children of men! Oh, that I could weep and mourn day and night for my sins and the sins of all people! Then I would weep blood, and flood the wounds of my Jesus with the blood of my <term>penitence</term><note>German: Buſſfertigkeit. # Preface

For everyone who seeks to hate the Devil’s kingdom, to leave behind the world and the created things <note>original: "Creatur"; refers to anything made by man or nature that distracts from the Creator</note>, and to gain a foothold in God’s kingdom: In one person, he <note>the Devil</note> stirs up the desire of the flesh for immorality; in another, he directs the reason and senses toward the worries of sustenance; in a third, he awakens a renewed desire for worldly business; in a fourth, for building, planting, and dwelling and burrowing with the heart inside created things; in a fifth, to marry and have a wife of his own, so that he might indulge his lust; in a sixth, he stirs up spiritual pride and secret—as well as public—arrogance, to boast of his gifts and show himself off before men; in the seventh, a false light and inner voices. And in such ways, through many thousands of methods, he has more inspirations to tempt the godly person, to see if he might bind and catch him perhaps with a rope, or often just with a thread, and thereafter further and further, until he has finally completely fooled him and occupied and possessed him with his influences and inspirations.

For I have now been writing on this for almost an entire year: because I only ever wrote something for myself now and then for my own edification <term>Erbauung: the process of spiritual "building up" or personal growth</term>, and had no intention of publishing anything further in print; however, I have now been compelled to do so by divine command and impulse for the common good. 

The content is, as already mentioned, actually and principally a presentation and detailed account of the **various states within man according to the three principles** <note>The "three principles" likely refers to the mystical cosmology of Jakob Böhme, involving the dark world, the light world, and the external world</note>: in part, as can be seen, these are **meditations** and **contemplations** of various—though often similar or closely related—matters, all of which aim toward the same purpose. Therefore, not everything follows a continuous, rigid order. Indeed, rather...

# Preface.

also, because these meditations have been left exactly as they welled up in the mind and flowed into the pen at various times and in different ways. Even though some are, so to speak, almost identical to one another or appear to be so, and deal with the same subject, a frequent repetition of one thing or another could not be avoided. Such things might perhaps arouse annoyance and disgust in an accurate and precise reader—one who is not accustomed to adjusting his judgment according to the scale of modesty and moderation, or who is otherwise easily thrown into a confusion of the senses due to a lack of the wisdom of the spirit. Through such wisdom, as a true <term>Artist</term> <note>original: "Artista"; likely referring to an alchemical or spiritual practitioner</note>, he should and would be able to understand the true <term>Art of Separation</term> <note>original: "Scheide-Kunst"; a term for alchemy or chemistry, here used metaphorically for spiritual discernment</note> and practice it in patience with a quiet spirit. In the meantime, one will nevertheless find that very little, or perhaps nothing, has been repeated without something different or further being noted alongside it. Not to mention that through this, a clear explanation is sometimes given for that which one could perhaps not express so clearly elsewhere or at another time; as an intelligent reader will perceive without effort, and—as I hope—will not be able to deny the good treasure that lies hidden in this little work. But as for him for whom it should not be useful to read, I would have him asked—

Firstly, the Dragon is the coarse, sectarian spirit of the world in all unconverted people / but especially in the high-ranking and the <term>Scribes</term> <note>original: "Schrifft-Gelehrten"; refers to those who study the letter of the law or scripture without spiritual illumination</note>. After that, the Dragon is a father of sins and of false powers and evil workings. Furthermore, the Dragon is a gnawing worm of sin / which does not die / and a fire / that is not extinguished. Furthermore, the Dragon is the highest <term>tincture</term> <note>In alchemy, a tincture is a spiritualized essence or transformative force</note> and active fire-power of all false fire-powers in spirits and souls: Yes, he is the wild fire of all evil magical spirits and men. The activity of the wild fire is a dragon: the activity of the false lights is a dragon. Further, the Dragon is the angry / reluctant / hostile hatred / stubbornness and self-will / working with great wrathful intensity and bitterness against all good and truth / which often greatly distresses the soul and makes it doubt the grace of God / especially the soul / which stands in the <term>Birth of Grace</term> <note>The process of spiritual rebirth or regeneration</note> / so that it often [would have to] despair—

# Concerning the Dragon.

...would have to be, if God were not their comfort in His grace and love. He is, furthermore, the spirit of all lie-working powers, the spirit of fornication, the spirit of sins.

If you ask: what kind of form, nature, and properties does he have? **Answer:** Often a very subtle spiritual fire-form, entirely poisoned with wrath and malice. If you now ask: where does he have his dwelling? **Answer:** In the great and small, dark, deep, sevenfold star-world. If you ask: what is the sevenfold star-world in the great deep? **Answer:** It is the great, wide, and broad-stretched dark abyss; as can be seen in the lower figure of the copperplate engraving <note>Refers to an illustration in the original printed book</note>: the great, dark, monstrous beast-kingdom of all spirits and souls of the wrath of God; the mother of all evil spirits, who perfectly possesses the four great elemental abysses, with the seven dark star-planets, the four great elements of the abyss—which abyss is also within every unregenerate human being.

### Concerning
# the Seven Heads
## of the Dragon.

**What are the seven heads of the dragon?**
->**Answer:**<-

They are seven subtle source-spirits <note>original: "Quell-Geister," a term from Jacob Böhme's theosophy referring to the fundamental forces of nature</note> of the seven dark planets, which work and well up into the wide-stretched souls, and possess the four great dark elements of the lowest abyss, and work in fire, water, air, and earth.

# Concerning the Dragon.

Furthermore, the seven heads of the dragon are seven evil angelic dominions <note>original: "englische Herrschafften" - referring to the hierarchy of fallen angels</note> or lordly powers of false activity; they are seven false planetary powers. These are seven kings of the forces that manifest themselves to do battle whenever a soul seeks to escape, acting as spirits of the devils and of the error of Belial <note>A Hebrew term for "worthlessness" or "wickedness," often personified as a demon or the devil himself</note>. Just as the dragon himself is a fiery, sectarian spirit of bickering and strife within the law of wrath, and is the clinging, sinful zeal of impurity; so too are his seven heads seven false spirits of error in the great and small world <note>The macrocosm (the universe) and the microcosm (the individual human being)</note>. In the great world, they are these spirits themselves; in the small world, they are their sons, who possess their nature and characteristics and are seven source-spirits <note>original: "Quell-Geister" - a term from Jacob Böhme's theosophy referring to the fundamental qualities or "fountains" of being</note> just like them. And these spirits are powerful through the "Great Ones" of the world for pouring out evil; but especially through the scripture-learned sectarian teachers. These scripture-learned colonels of the world are themselves also dragons and beasts through the sectarian dead letter <note>A reference to literalist religious interpretation that lacks spiritual life</note>, with which they defend themselves as if with the defenses and weapons of the devil. 

However, these seven kings are also otherwise present in all unregenerate <note>original: "unwiedergebohrnen" - those who have not undergone a spiritual rebirth or "New Birth"</note> people, namely as the active <term>Astral Spirit</term> <note>original: "Stern-Geist" - the influence of the stars and planets upon the human soul and temperament</note> which wells up within them all malice and sin: evil thoughts, murder, adultery, fornication, theft, false witness, blasphemy, and the like; avarice and lies, pride and unchastity, gluttony and drunkenness. All of this is done by the active power of the seven astral spirits of the dragon, who work a great sea full of sin wherever they dwell. For just as one water flows into another, and all waters run together into the sea and accumulate in the sea: so one sin flows into another—which these seven source-spirits work—and one malice into another; and all these accumulate so that a great

### Concerning
# the ten horns
## of the Dragon.

### What are the ten horns of the Dragon?

Answer: They are ten royal law-spirits that proceed from the Dragon and his heads, acting as the ten commandment-angels who introduce sin by the Devil's command through the law of one's own self-will. And these are his ten power-spirits of war and strife, which resist the ten power- and strength-angels of the holy law of God in the small and large world <note>The "small and large world" refers to the Microcosm (man) and the Macrocosm (the universe)</note>. For through these the Devil introduces sin, and fights against those who are enemies of sin by means of his ten horns, which are the strength- and power-spirits of sin, and the ten war-spirits, kings, and army-commanders in the inner world over the ten powers of the sin-spirits who rule in the air of the inner world. They are leaders of the sin-spirits, whom they lead against the hosts of those who keep God's law when the fiery Dragon becomes angry against the holy soul; and through these ten power-horns of sin, he occupies his inner fortresses—namely, everything he possesses within the "abyssal man" <term>Abgrunds-Mensch: the aspect of the human being rooted in the dark, chaotic depths of the spiritual abyss</term> of the dark world.

### Concerning the
# Red Dragon in Man
### in particular.

Egypt—the first head—originated from the fiery sea-dragon, which had seven crowned heads and was allied with and embodied by the ten horns of the ten nations of Canaan. For Egypt and Canaan were inhabited by the children of Ham after the Great Flood. Now, Ham signifies sin, or the root of sins: for Ham means a Moor <note>original: "Mor"; here used historically to denote a dark-skinned person, symbolizing spiritual darkness</note> or a black, dark person. And thus, sin is nothing but darkness and gloom. When it is attacked and stirred up within a person, it becomes fiery, red, and angry; but when it remains unchallenged and everything goes according to its desire, it is always darkness and gloom. For it does not come to the light, but remains lying in the darkness and gloom of its own corruption, and grows continually into an even greater heap: just as Ham fathered Canaan; that is: the root brought forth a tree, and the tree ten branches, and the ten branches innumerable twigs, leaves, and evil fruits, so that all who ate from this tree had to die. These are the ten nations of Canaan, the ten horns of the red dragon—namely, the indwelling sin of the heart, which fights against God and the holy Israel, against love and truth. # Of the Beast.

The word of gentleness <note>German: *Sanfftmüthigkeit*; implies a humble, yielding spirit</note> winds around the beast with the ten horns of strife: namely, the sectarians then fall away along with all their opinions; and love, as the word of gentleness, enters into the government in place of sectarian words and quarrelsome laws. And the horns, which are broken through death and overcome unto life, give their kingdom to the Lamb of gentleness, who thereafter uses the laws of God for the benefit of the soul with all the might and power that follow God’s law. And then the law of God gives and teaches the virtues that follow into eternal life for him who keeps them; and His commandments are not burdensome once they have come into life within the soul. 

But this cannot happen until Moses—the Spirit of God—has broken the first tablets of the law with the ten words <note>The Ten Commandments</note> which they have transgressed, and gives a new commandment into their heart and mind that is living. This is indeed the same old commandment; but the letter <note>The literal, academic understanding of scripture</note> with its scholarship must be removed from the brain, and a new foundation must be laid, where one may no longer sin against the commandment while relying on grace—as if buying on credit <note>German: *auf die lange Kreiden*; an idiom for delaying payment or taking advantage of credit</note>—but instead acts according to what is written: **He who keeps it shall live.** For God gave it for life and not for death; for the obedience of a virtuous life and not for disobedience. For the ten truths of virtue come through the law of God’s life, which are like a Tabernacle of God <note>German: *Hütte Gottes*; referring to the biblical Tabernacle in the wilderness</note> with ten curtains artistically woven into one another, made of pure silk; these are the following righteousnesses in the holy powers of the soul, where all kinds of joyful, powerful angels and Cherubic Sabbath- and

### Of the
# False Prophet.
## What then is this beast that has risen out of the earth?

->Answer:<-

It is the false prophet, who was previously one with the Beast, but subsequently departs from it and begins to testify against it, rejecting the dead letter <note>The "dead letter" refers to a purely intellectual or literal understanding of scripture without spiritual life.</note> and inflicting a wound upon it through the sword of the Spirit. This occurs because the first law of the letter had only entered into the knowledge, and not into the essence, of man; meanwhile, they continually danced around their calves <note>A reference to the Golden Calf in Exodus, symbolizing idolatry.</note> and performed the works of the devil. Thus, Israel receives another tablet of stone after Moses punished the first sins and shattered their scholarship—the dead letter in the brain. However, they transgress these as well, and sin against these stone tablets just as they did with the previous ones. And this is the Beast of Judah with the two horns, which clings to Israel, which has the ten horns. For the first, which transgressed and was transgressed against, signifies Israel: it was the first to fall away from the House of David and from the Law, which the Lord afterwards rejected. These ten horns have entered into the flesh according to the threefold ground of nature's corruption: where each of the three grounds is based upon ten heights of the "sciences" <note>original: "Wissenschafften," here meaning worldly or intellectual systems of knowledge that lead away from spiritual truth.</note> that lead to destruction. Thus, the three-times-ten are thirty high horns: and each horn is called a "measure" that is one cubit long. With these, the outer man of the flesh, the middle man of the soul's sensuality, and the inner man of the heart—the spiritual source-soul—are measured. 

So these three souls are measured with the thirty measuring-rods, which are named Ignorance, Lack of Understanding, and Unbelief. With these three-times-ten rods, they measure the souls as if through the Law, and begin to build one loose foundation upon another, until the building in the soul becomes thirty-times-ten cubits high, according to the trinity of the worker and that which is worked upon. # Transformation into the Lamb.

Since man has allowed the three-hundred-fold root of the tree of wickedness to take root within himself, which hangs full of the fruits of sin, God has given three hundred commandments of life through his spiritual Moses. He speaks the ten primary Words <note>The Ten Commandments</note> orally to the sinner through ten powerful thunders against his heart, in order to break him and awaken him according to the Spirit. And these are God's living horns of power, which must strive within us against the rebellious ones, as ten living powers of God which He writes livingly into us as opposites through His Spirit. This Spirit must become for us a fire of God and a hammer that shatters the rocks.

And then He allows the Gospel to be preached to the ten cities, which previously walked according to the flesh with their inhabitants—the evil lusts and desires in the flesh—and resisted the law of God. But now, because the Word of Gentleness <note>original: "Sanfftmuht"</note> is revealed with its mighty voice of thunder, it sprinkles pure water into them, so that they become clean from all impurity. This is the water and fire through which everything must pass, be washed, and be purified, if it wishes to come to God. For the ten commandments of God demand a perfect obedience: they kill and strike the sinner down, and make him leprous like snow <note>A reference to the biblical account of Miriam or Gehazi, symbolizing the outward manifestation of internal sin</note>, until they perfectly convince him of his sins and misdeeds.

And for so long the angry Lamb <note>A paradoxical image of Christ as both the sacrificial lamb and the righteous judge</note> strives within them as a word of rebirth and new creation, until it has perfectly overcome them. Then it heals the leprosy of sins: and there appear ten virgins, who begin to adorn their lamps in the Sanctuary, and become like ten golden candlesticks, such as were in the Temple of Solomon.

# Of Spiritual Sea-Vessels

...namely the holy Seed of Power of Israel, which has come to the light within the soul and has entered into the Ark of the Covenant of the little Ship of Grace of Jesus Christ. And although they are indeed tempted and attacked by sea-robbers <note>likely referring to demonic distractions or worldly temptations</note>, they are nevertheless safe within it, and shall by no means perish before the stormy weather of the evil spirits, which like a Great Flood <note>original: "Sündfluht"</note> fall upon all who are in the little Ship of the Cross. For they are preserved by Him whom the wind and sea, along with its waves, must obey; they cannot perish because the Lord Himself is their Captain and holds the rudder. Therefore, their course continues nonetheless, even though the sea rages and seethes; indeed, it is often furthered all the more by this, so that with a rich cargo they reach the Port of Rest so much the faster.

Namely, the good senses, holy desires, and blameless thoughts, which have entered into the covenant of a good conscience with God; these shall sail into the heights and depths with their little Covenant-Ship, upon which the **red Cross-Banner** is always hoisted as a sign of victory. They shall find great wealth, namely the most precious pearls, the finest gold, the best gemstone of **Humility**, the bright **Jasper** of beautiful virtues, along with the **Sardius** <note>a reddish gemstone, often associated in mystical texts with the blood of Christ or divine love</note> of surrender and serenity <note>original: "Gelassenheit," a key term in German mysticism referring to the state of letting go of self-will</note>. 

They also find the eternally-pulling **Magnetic Power** of the Father, who powerfully draws their active Magnet of Faith to Himself through the working power of Christ which is within them—especially when they hold the active **Rudder of Faith** in the hands of firm confidence and hope, and therewith work stiffly and incessantly through storm and waves in the unfathomable depths. And so they [follow] Christ, the Word of the new creation, with his new kings of birth, the 144,000 <note>A reference to the redeemed in the Book of Revelation</note>, who [follow] the Lamb of God upon white

# The Spiritual Sea-Combat
## on the inward Sea of the Soul.

When the little spiritual Covenant-Ship <note>original: "Bundes-Schifflein" — a metaphor for the soul or the community of believers bound by a covenant with God</note> with its inhabitants of God travels into the heights and depths in the spiritual Sea of Senses, it is subject to much danger: especially when the pirate ship of Reason <note>original: "Raubschiff der Vernunfft" — the author views purely human logic as a predator to faith</note> or the fleshly privateers with their evil lusts and desires come upon them, and thus storm against the Covenant-Ship in the spiritual Sea of the Soul. Then they often become anxious and fearful, so that the Spirit admonishes them and speaks to them in the danger: Here are those who keep God's commandment and the faith in Jesus! Here is the enduring patience of the Saints! And because the four great impetuous winds then storm—namely the Dragon, the old Serpent, the Devil, and Satan—upon the sea, the sun of the Law often sets, and the sun withholds its shine along with the Light of Grace.

<summary>This page uses maritime allegory to describe the soul's total reliance on God's Spirit and the subsequent spiritual warfare it faces against human reason and demonic forces. It emphasizes that while the "Covenant-Ship" of the soul faces storms and "pirates" of lust, divine protection ensures the safety of the faithful.</summary>
<keywords>spiritual navigation, Covenant-Ship, Sea of the Soul, Reason vs. Faith, spiritual warfare, Holy Spirit, patience of saints</keywords>

## GOD and the Soul,
### according to their Essence and Root.

God—the eternal, unchangeable, omnipotent, great, and high Majesty—is in His essential beginning (though there is no beginning or end, nor shall there be) a threefold Being, which has revealed itself as God the Father, God the Son, and God the Holy Spirit, before, in, and after the creation of the entire visible and invisible world.

# Of GOD.

I say: even before the creation. For when the Majesty of God still dwelt within itself in the single eternal Being, and had not yet revealed itself in or through any single creature, it was already in this Triune Being <note>original: "DreyEinigen Wesen"</note>, and worked through it, and brought itself forth in the source-spring of light <note>The author uses "Quall" or "Quelle" to describe a bubbling, active fountain of divine energy</note>, within itself, when it had not yet brought forth anything outside of itself.

These, however, are the three Primary Beings <note>or "Principal Essences"</note> in which God has expressed Himself from eternity, and has also revealed Himself in time within the creature.

Firstly, the Being of all beings is entirely without beginning, a self-working and uncreated thing, dwelling within itself and proceeding from itself, an everlasting source-being, a God without God, without father and without mother. For He Himself is the all-powerful, essential light, without birth, without being wrought by other lights. For in Himself God is fire, and outside Himself He is light, all of which is one Being, and born of itself, as an ever-welling eternal fountain of life, which from the past eternities into the yet future eternities has neither beginning nor end. For He is and remains in a single and eternal outflowing and inflowing, as the single God-Being that has constantly flowed out from the eternities and flows back again into the eternities. He is an eternal light, which no mortal creature has seen or can see with eyes, nor touch with hands, nor comprehend with reason, nor recognize with an impure heart: For it is far too pure, clear, and piercing with its rays, such as no sun in the heaven can be. For the sources and offspring of the light through the Spirit, which is God, are entirely incomprehensible in their depths and heights, breadths and lengths, which pour themselves in full radiance from the sources of the eternities into the

# Of GOD

Depths and heights in all places. Indeed, the springs of the great depths are unfathomable, which proceed from God into God, and ever raise the true essence <note>original: "Wesen" — refers to the fundamental nature or being of a thing</note> higher into the most perfect essence, which continually elevates itself from power to power, from light into light and the most perfect clarities, in the eternities of eternities. Thus the eternal essence stands in its center <note>original: "Centrum"</note> and does not depart from its circle <note>original: "Circul"</note>, nor does it become weaker when it begins to age: for it cannot age, because it gives birth to itself anew forever and ever. Although time begins to age, the power of God nevertheless upholds it always, so that it stands there like a blooming youth, fresh, joyful, and very fruitful, through the ever-regenerating source-spirit <note>original: "Quell-Geist"</note> of God, which is very piercing in the births of the eternally reborn of God, and creates great heights and depths, all around full of the great glory of God.

Thus the unoriginated essence of God is without beginning, without origin, and remains without end; it does not begin to grow old in all eternities, and creates everything anew in all creatures that are created in heaven and on earth and under the earth, through the eternal Wisdom <note>original: "Weißheit"</note>, which is the living, in-born Word of God, Christ, the incorruptible seed-grain <note>original: "Saamen-Korn"</note> through which all things are created, and which must now be received in all creatures who are to come to the true new birth. For the old man—sin—must die, and the new Christ-man must rise up, grow, live, rule, and remain in us. Through him, we, together with heaven and earth, angels, and all visible and invisible creatures, heavenly or earthly, are created. For everything comes from God through the spoken, in-born

# Of Four Kinds of Souls.

...ness in *Christ Jesus* and the Holy Spirit / Amen!
->Hallelujah! Amen!<-

### Concerning the
## Origin and Creation
## of Souls
### in General.

In the beginning, GOD created four kinds of souls / and bodies for them / which were the heads of all souls; namely: the whale or *Leviathan* <note>A primordial sea monster mentioned in the Old Testament, often symbolizing chaos.</note> in the depths of the waters; the *Behemoth* / as the somewhat purer beast / upon the earth; the serpent in Paradise; and the divine human also in Paradise.

These four kinds of souls, according to their nature, are prefigured for us and represent four kinds of persons at the crucifixion of *Christ* / and with them four kinds of people. Namely: *Barabbas* / the worst murderer and rebel / resembles the water-beast / which swims in the depths of all vice and malice with the sea-dragons. The thief at the left hand of *Christ* represents the serpent / which is also an evil and a cunning, unclean beast / and is likewise punished for false deeds. This, however, represents the outward *Pharisaic* <note>Referring to the Pharisees; here used to describe those who are hypocritically self-righteous.</note> nature of men / who do not wish to be scolded as being so very evil / and yet in deed are evil and against *Christ*. For although they allow themselves to be bound with *Christ* to the cross / that is / to the outward statutes / it is nevertheless only hypocrisy with them. The third kind of people is prefigured for us by the *Behemoth* and the thief at the right hand of *Christ*. The *Behemoth* is a coarse and stupid beast /

Not for the sake of his own guilt in and of himself, but for our sake and for the sake of the world's sin, which he has taken upon himself, he must die on the Cross as the Mediator and Reconciler. # Of the Leviathan.

God took [this] in the beginning of the divine works, as is said of the **Behemoth** in *Job 40:10, 14* <note>original: "Hiob XL, 10. 14." In modern Bibles, this corresponds to Job 40:15-19.</note>, that he is the beginning of the ways of God. And although the soul of **Behemoth** is called here the initial soul in the animal kingdom, it is not the meaning that the animal Earth-soul was created before the **Leviathanic** Water-soul: Not at all! Rather, God only wants to say so much to *Job*, that He created this **Behemoth-like** animal soul before the rational **Adamite** soul, but not before the **Leviathanic** dragon- and water-soul. God also paints this [Leviathan] for *Job* in the most gruesome manner, showing how cruel it is, so that nothing can be compared to it, and how strong and mighty the psychic fire- and dragon-kingdom is in the abyss of the depths of the sea. God made this during the fifth day's work <note>The fifth day of the Creation week in Genesis.</note> and created it out of the depths, namely out of the impure fire-constellation of the initially-fallen dragon, who fell away from God when God had made the angelic world <note>original: "engliſche Welt." In this context, "engliſch" refers to angels (Engel), not the English nation.</note>. For this **Leviathan-soul** is an outflow from the initial dragon, the first branch of his being, the son of his entire nature, the state-soul <term>Staats-Seele: likely referring to a governing or collective soul that organizes a specific realm of existence</term> and mother of all devilish powers and out-born souls, who swim with their father the Devil in the depths of all vices, and were conceived, born, and raised by the dragon. # Of the Leviathan.

# On the Origin
## of the Behemoth-like Animal Souls
### *in the Macrocosm and Microcosm.*

The great and small Behemoth-like <note>Behemoth: here used to represent the primal, earthly, and monstrously physical nature of the fallen world</note> natural animal and human soul originates from the darkness; namely, from the fire-world and the impure outflow of the heated stars <note>Gestirn: refers to the astrological influence of the celestial bodies on human temperament</note>.
Therefore, it is now to be asked:
->What then is the soul, properly speaking?<-
Answer: The soul is nothing other than the natural fire of life in animals and humans: specifically, a threefold branch in the complete animals, and a threefold branch in the animalistic human.
From where shall one take a likeness <note>Gleichniß: a parable or metaphorical comparison</note> that explains the origin of souls to us? Answer: I know of no better likeness to present at this time than that of a stone. For just as the fire is hidden within a natural stone and is not seen, so too is the soul-life of nature hidden in the tree of the dark stars, as if in a hard stone; and it cannot be brought forth until the male and the female join together and attend to the stars of desire, and the male hardness—like the spirit of nature—comes to the aid of the female tinder of soft, natural fleshly love, so that the hardness of the steel

# On the Image of GOD

If the tree is good, then the limbs and branches are also good, along with their fruit. For by the fruit, one recognizes the tree. <note>A reference to Matthew 7:17-20, a common motif in mystical literature to explain how internal spiritual states manifest in outward actions.</note>

This is the soulful, astral Tree of Life, which stands planted within the threefold human being—initially by the hand of **GOD** and through the astral realm of nature: namely, the threefold soul in man: a masculine, a feminine, and an animal soul, located in the seed, the brain, and the heart; of which more is said elsewhere. <note>The "astral" or "starred" (gestirnte) nature refers to the influence of the celestial bodies on the physical and emotional temperament of man.</note>

These are now the works which **GOD** made all good in the Beginning: but through the apostasy <note>The Fall of Man</note> from **GOD**, they have become evil. For **GOD** initially subjected all things—which He made in the third, fifth, and sixth day's work—to the new Adam and made them into a subservient servant: so that the fiery Adam, swimming in the depths of all malice, and the earthly Adam of the earth, should serve and be obedient to the spiritual Adam and his wife, as the twofold Image of **GOD**.

->Concerning the<-

And the Light-Queen **Hevah** was to stand within her **Adam Christ**, the Sun, from whom she was taken—as if from a yet higher and greater Light-King—and be governed and brought back by him. And **Christ** was to stand in **GOD**, the even much greater Light-King, and be born and brought back by Him. And **GOD** was to stand in the Spirit, the highest of all lights, and bring Himself forth higher and higher, and ever higher still, and make His name glorious. For **GOD** is the Spirit, and the Lamb, and the Love: and these three are one.

Everything that **GOD** created in the beginning is good, and nothing is to be rejected, whether it be in heaven or on the earth. For **God** created the animals and fish good in the beginning, just as He did the angels and men; but each one in its own order. **God** made nothing disorderly, but rather everything quite orderly. **God** did not create the animals with the intention that men should become animalistic and of the same mind as the beasts, and be the servants of animals: the **Devil** did that. But **God** in the beginning made the animals and fish subject to man, so that the animals should learn human customs <note>original: "Sitten," referring to ways of behaving, morals, or character</note> and be governed by men, and be the sacrifice and food of men. But men were not to learn animalistic customs; rather, the

Of this seed, man was meant to eat in a magical manner <note>In this context, "magical" refers to a spiritual or supernatural process of transformation rather than stage magic.</note>, and bring forth the powers of the seed in the Light, so that they might be ripened in the Holy Virgin <note>Likely referring to the "Sophia" or Divine Wisdom, a common figure in Boehme-influenced mysticism</note>, and through her be born into God.

For in this way, birth was intended to happen according to the order of GOD. Behemoth <note>original: "Behemoth"; here representing the raw, animalistic drive or the "mother-desire" of the physical world</note>, as the mother-desire with its seed, was meant to give birth to the Eve <note>original: "Hevah"</note> of man in an animal-like fashion. This Eve, however, was meant to give birth to her Adam—the Eve of GOD—and thus make the animal-like qualities human. And Adam—that is, God’s Eve—was meant to bring forth the human powers divinely from himself, so that they would become children of God. Therefore, GOD created and made these three parts in one day during the sixth creation, so that the three sixes <note>The number 666, interpreted here as a structural progression of nature, humanity, and divinity rather than purely as an evil mark</note> should always proceed with their offspring. That is: The mother-animal Behemoth, which brought forth the outer powers in an animal way in the first number-six, was meant to give birth higher, indeed entirely humanly, in Eve, the second number-six; and through the third number-six, Adam—as the mediator between GOD and man—was to give birth divinely into the number-seven, as if into God’s rest.

Over the female animal-power, the Behemoth-like Church, Adam and Eve were appointed by GOD as lords, to rule over her and over all her animals of desire and reason upon earth, because they had received only a small night-light, like the stars, within themselves during the creation, and that indeed from the sun and moon.

<summary>This page describes a mystical hierarchy of birth where animal nature (Behemoth) is transformed into human nature (Eve) and finally into divine nature (Adam), corresponding to a progression through the symbolic numbers six and seven. It establishes humanity's intended dominion over the lower "animal" impulses of reason and desire.</summary>

# On the Number of Man

But Ham—that is, the entirely dark light of reason, indeed the serpent or the Prince of Darkness—was made by GOD into a servant of all servants with 69 offspring. Instead, he had to go into the sea of the deeps; that is: the stellar powers of the reason-serpents were to be hidden in the deep, thick darkness, so that they would not come before the eyes of men, but remained hidden in their dark, lowest stellar element of the waters. Meanwhile, the triple-six—namely the animalistic Behemoth <note>A massive land beast from the Book of Job, here representing the lower physical nature</note>, man’s wife, and man himself—who were called out from the depth of the waters (that is, out of the darkness) and were created by GOD in the sixth day’s work as beast, half-human, and whole human, were now intended to be born into an even higher divine and spiritual degree in GOD. This is in the *Ruach* <note>Hebrew: "Spirit" or "Breath"</note> or the rest of the Holy Spirit and of the New Jerusalem, which came as a holy out-breathing from the mouth of GOD into the God-image of the six-fold man, Adam.

For after this triune divine man had received the sixth creation-number in three different ways—namely in an animal manner, a feminine manner, and a masculine manner—GOD breathed into his nostrils the *Ruach* or Spirit of eternal life in the seventh day’s work, so that man should be a living soul who had the life of GOD within himself. For GOD wishes to rest in His rest in the seventh day’s work. But His rest is the Spirit or in-breathing which He [breathed] into the soul...

# Perversion

It let go of his Eve—that is, the **natural Adam**—who previously had been clothed with the divine birth as if with the **light of the sun**, and held the **moon** (his Eve) beneath him, so that he was obedient not to her, but to **God**, who had gifted him the divine birth and wrapped him in it like a garment.

In the same way, he had held beneath him the power of his woman: the **seven-headed red dragon** <note>A reference to Revelation 12:3, interpreted here as the chaotic astral forces</note> who had always tempted him to rob him of the divine birth, that beautiful **Sun-Child**, the holy masculine virgin <note>German: *männliche Jungfrau*; a mystical concept of the original androgynous spiritual state</note> who was born of **God** and was **God's** offering through the bringing forth of **Adam**, so that the dragon could not overcome him. # Concerning the Number of the Beast

The understanding of man within the senses—wherein Satan has bored the 72 false lights—was entirely confused and led astray.

Thus, although man built the Star-Tower <note>A reference to the Tower of Babel</note> up to the very heavens with the 72 chief kingdoms of the false constellations—which were bored into the senses like 72 channels and steps—he nevertheless could not climb into heaven. Instead, he remained forever in the dark constellations with his understanding, and within the 72 lights of reason, which nonetheless could not enlighten him. And even if he had bored another 72 in addition, so that there were 144, and had become just like his father the Devil in the lowest star-kingdom of the abyss, he still could not have reached Paradise again, except through true repentance of the heart and a change of mind <note>original: "Sinnes-Aenderung"; a translation of the Greek *metanoia*</note>. Through these, he had to climb back down again and forget what reason had fabricated, being led to Paradise by another path that is entirely unknown to the earthly Adam.

This, however, is the path of humility, which is bored into the understanding toward Paradise. Upon this path, man must now be brought through Babel—that is, through a seventy-fold star-kingdom of confusion—as a suffering, poor, imprisoned Adam. In this Babel, he must be hated, mocked, imprisoned, and even entirely killed in his inner state by the eight-times-nine or 72 births of Cain. For he must descend from his high senses and give leave to the false night-lights in the brain, which previously led him away from God. He must give leave to the evil thoughts in the heart-man <note>The inner spiritual self</note>, which are like those evil beasts and serpents he followed as false inspirations and suggestions.

# On the Number of the Beast.

For often in the spring, and even late into the season, we receive snow and cold; which is nothing other than the false prophet who deceives the world, giving it winter instead of summer by raising up again the starry images of *Venus* and *Mars*, which in the beginning had stirred up destruction.

These, then, are the internal and external starry realms which are guided from the Dragon and the Beast—that is, from the *body* <note>original: "Corpus"</note> of the entire fiery constellation—through the 70 or 72 Star-Kings into the inner and outer world, in order to establish the throne and the number of the beast.

### Concerning the
## Divine-Lawful Number
# Proceeding from the Number of the Beast.

This is the triple <term>Human-Number</term> <note>The author refers to a symbolic numerical value assigned to humanity in its redeemed state</note>, which now begins to emerge again from the <term>Serpent-Number</term> and <term>Beast-Number</term> through a true change of heart and mind. Thus, the kingdom of the beast and the serpent in Adam—which stands in this figure (5555)—is now transformed into the sixth <term>Creation-Number</term> (6666). Humanity now begins to leave the fifth number behind in the outer and inner realms and departs from it.

However, because of this, a great struggle and war is stirred up within the <term>divine-lawful man</term> <note>A person living according to God's laws</note> to fight against the serpent-realm; and indeed, the longer it lasts, the more violent it becomes. # On the God-Ordained Number

->judge according to the gift that he has received from God!<- It would be disparaging to our God if He were to let Himself be measured according to human standards. This is certainly true: as many droplets as there are in the rain, so manifold is God’s gift and blessing; as many members of Christ as there are here on earth, so manifoldly does God allow His blessing and His gifts to be distributed! We are fools and simpletons that we wish to teach and convert one another in such a way with our own gift, or to force one’s gift upon another as if he were greater than God! I do not venture to master another in his gift, whether it be small or large. If he presents his gift as good, and it is not good, no one but he himself will suffer the harm. If he presents it as gold, and it is no gold, the Day <note>A reference to the Day of Judgment or the "Day of the Lord" mentioned in 1 Corinthians 3:13</note> will make it clear when he is led through the fire with his gift, whether it was gold, or dross and scum. I, for my part, have been sufficiently convicted of my faults by my God, even if no human being told me where I had failed. For God knows and recognizes the hearts of men best; therefore, He can also best convict them when they err and stumble. Everything that I, on my part, write and have written, I do not wish to have forced upon anyone as an article of faith or doctrine <term>Glaubens- und Lehr-Artickel: formal dogmas or essential tenets of a church</term>; rather, because it was given to me as a gift, I wish to share it in turn as a gift with those who, like me, are perhaps eager to take something from it for their benefit, whatever is useful and necessary for each person. But I do not wish to have it forced upon anyone who perhaps has a different calling <term>Ruff: a spiritual vocation or internal summons</term> from God than to receive gifts that he perhaps cannot yet grasp or comprehend. For if I wanted to force it upon such a person by power,

## Of the Divine Number.

Just as it happens here in the little **Soul-World**, which has entered perfectly into **God** and his **Sabbath** and rest, and possesses a **thousand-year kingdom** <term>Millennium: a period of spiritual perfection and peace</term> within the **thrice-holy God**, after it has been brought to completion through the seven **Creation-Spirits** of **God** into the **7000**: So likewise shall a **thousand-year kingdom** of rest in **God** be in the future for the general, great **World-Adam** <note>The "World-Adam" refers to the collective humanity or the macrocosm.</note>, who has hitherto departed from **God** and fallen back into the **Beast- and Serpent-Kingdom**; when **God** shall have visited him with very great, general **World-Plagues** and terrible judgments for his apostasy and disobed—

# Of the Divine Number.

**God**, after the punishment and chastisement, will have mercy upon the remaining **Adam** <note>The author uses "Adam" here as a collective term for all of humanity.</note> / and will, amidst the judgment / as an **Adam** banished by the Law / have him called back and invited to Himself through the universal Gospel of **Christ**. And then he will draw near to **God** / recognize and confess his guilt and transgression / bow before **God** / and seek and receive grace. Thus the universal **Adam** will for a long time enter into marriage with **God** and **Christ** / according to the Law of **God** / and thus live according to God's law upon the whole earth, just as the first universal **Israel** did in the time of **David** and **Solomon**.

However, after this, the time shall be changed for him again / and the last beast with the two horns <note>A reference to the second beast of Revelation 13:11, often interpreted in this period as a false prophet or corrupt ecclesiastical power.</note> will bring the first one up again / for seventy years.

And after that time / when **God** shall have once again held judgment over the fallen-back universal **Adam** / the true universal seventh day-work of the rest of **God** <note>The "Sabbath rest" or the Millennial kingdom.</note> will reveal itself in all men / and **Adam** will then generally begin to penetrate into the great **Seven-Creation-Number** / as into the seventh **Millennium**. And that is then the universal **Evangelical Grace** / which will befall all **Peoples** / from the Gospel of peace and the love of **Jesus Christ** the Son of **God**. For all **Peoples** will arise in this number 7777 of the seven spirits of **God** / penetrate into them / be wedded with them and be incorporated into them; and will then, from the beginning of the 7000th until the end of the seventh **Millennium** / stand in a very good condition externally and internally.

# Of the Tree of Life. 101

Christ is the root, and God’s Spirit is the power, sap, and life. God’s Wisdom is a wellspring of understanding and an eternally flowing source full of all kinds of knowledge of God. Where the knowledge of God is, there is life and blessing; and a sanctified, understanding heart is filled with it. The knowledge of God is a tree of life, and its root is the source of understanding: whoever eats from it shall live forever, and whoever drinks from it will always long for more.

O, how lovely it is when a person becomes pious! For he shall be like a well-watered land that is constantly rained upon, whereon all kinds of beautiful, fruitful trees grow. Even if they are of a lowly trunk <note>meaning humble or modest in stature</note>, they are nonetheless rich with all kinds of beautiful fruits which the Lord has planted in their heart. The land of the fear of God is full of all kinds of beautiful trees of the understanding of God, planted there. God’s Wisdom is the most beautiful tree that is in the Paradise of God: alongside it stand planted all kinds of beautiful trees of virtue, which are all of a very lowly trunk, but their fruit is lovely to eat.

Eat Wisdom, and drink, my dear friends, the wine of love! Practice mercy, plant humble lowliness, diligently water the little herb of patience, and practice gentleness! Eat of it always, and satisfy yourselves with all kinds of beautiful and green herbs of truth! Eat much of it, and let all the growths of outward-working righteousness be your food, like the herbs in the field! Everything that grows in the garden of God, eat that! Do not eat the weeds that are sown and propagated with serpent’s venom: whose root is called falsehood, and its grass and herbal-dung is untruth and lies, and its seed is malice; which stands planted in the sleep

->Of the<-
## Luciferian Tree of Temptation.

The Luciferian Tree of Temptation in the knowledge of good and evil is a tree of Paradise, and indeed the middle-most, highest, and most puffed-up among the trees. But its root has no ground in the earth of the inner Paradise, but rather in the corrupted world of nature. Its branches are called spiritual pride, false freedom in the appearance of good; and its foundation is magical science <note>The term "magische Wissenschafft" here refers to an esoteric or occult understanding of the hidden workings of nature and spirit</note> in light and darkness; and its height is proud clericalism and haughty imagination of oneself, the desire to be highly regarded, clever-mindedness, self-importance, and a great fondness for titles <note>original: "Titulirsamkeit"</note>. All who have come to Christ in the innermost center of Paradise through the magical working of faith must be tested by this ascending Tree of Temptation, especially those who have attained great knowledge through magical science. # Good and Evil.

Those who eat from the tree of self-importance, the desire to be well-regarded, and haughty conceit—contrary to the commandment and prohibition of God—must be cast out of Paradise again. For the root of this tall tree has not taken hold in the ground with the lowly trees in the Garden of Light, but rather in the dark heaps of earth outside the city. Therefore, the innermost Light-Paradise is locked away from him, as a proud spirit; and the key of the gentle David <note>A reference to the "Key of David" from Revelation 3:7, symbolizing spiritual authority and access to divine truth.</note> is taken away and extinguished through his arrogance. For the light is the gentle key; it yields and flees before the darkness. Therefore, they grope like the blind for the entrance, just as the Sodomites did before Lot’s house when they wanted to force and violate Wisdom <note>The author personifies the angels/visitors in the Sodom story as "Wisdom."</note> out of the house by violence; but they were struck with blindness by Wisdom, whom Lot had sheltered in silence. Just as those attackers could not find the entrance, so too these proud spirits cannot: because the entrance is a mystery and is only found by the simple ones who remain in lowliness with the lowly trees. These are the ones who, through the holy key of David—the Holy Spirit—open and shut the gate whenever they wish, and go in and out to the Tree of Life. For everything high must be brought low, so that the Lord alone may be high within us, from now until eternity.

Furthermore, this tree is also the tall Tree of Nourishment, from which many thousands have already eaten death against the prohibition of God: namely, all those who have again given ear to the crafty serpent of reason <note>The "serpent of reason" (Vernunfft-Schlange) is a common trope in radical pietism, suggesting that human logic without divine inspiration leads to spiritual fall.</note> to eat of the fruits of the tree. # Good and Evil

Whoever is negligent in the work of the Lord is an abomination both before God and men. He who is negligent has already placed the sword into the hands of the Tempter, so that he may tempt him. He who is negligent will be sifted <note>original: "reitern" - a term for sifting grain, here used metaphorically for spiritual testing</note>, and he will be sifted with all kinds of temptations until he finally falls back to the very ground and soil of Sodom. He who performs the commandment of life negligently shall be cursed, according to the Law of God.

Furthermore, it is to be noted regarding this Tree of Temptation that, although it is a fruitful tree, God has no pleasure in any of the fruits it brings forth, because it is a wild fig tree whose fruits are very evil and not to be eaten. For its fruits are tasteless and serve no one for food except the swine who strive after fleshly lusts. This forbidden tree is nothing other than the sloth of the Laodiceans <note>A reference to the "lukewarm" church in Revelation 3:14-22</note>, the cold and lukewarm Christians who mix good and evil together; who know much, but have nothing in their deeds; who always like to seat themselves at the head of the table with the traitor Judas; who esteem themselves more blessed than others and consider themselves rich and wise; who are much wiser, cleverer, and more understanding in their haughty minds than all others alongside them—who yet often have and possess a true light and knowledge of Jesus Christ within themselves. Indeed, they despise that which holds itself low and humble, and they turn the grace of Jesus Christ into wantonness, and speak as errant and "Free Spirits" <note>term: "Frey-Geister" - likely referring to the Radical Pietist or Antinomian movements that claimed spiritual liberty excused them from moral laws</note>: "Christ has won such freedom for us; we must not live so legally <note>i.e., strictly according to the letter of the law</note>; a Christian must be able to do everything; to the pure all things are pure; we cannot make our nature any different than God made it; committing fornication with the flesh is no sin"; and the like.

And just as resin and pitch pull everything toward themselves, so do all godless rulers and the powerful on earth draw land and people to themselves, whitewashing and pitching them together with their loose teachings so that they hold together as one body <note>original: "Corpus"</note>, just as pitch and grease hold together. Just as the dark constellations are full of active dark forces—where cold and heat, ice and snow, work through one another and bring forth fruit in the body of the dark earth—so the fleshly fire-soul and star-soul <note>The "star-soul" or "astral soul" refers to the part of man influenced by the cosmic and planetary forces of the material world.</note> bring forth their wickednesses through the flesh, so that they grow out through one another like a forest, and like thorns and hedges, they lay everything waste.

# The Tree of Man.

This refers to astrological influences which the author believes can lead the intellect astray if not grounded in God.</note> to the stargazers and the learned, so that they shall drink from it and become drunken, to woo and commit fornication with her. Literally "sin-flood," the German word for the Great Flood of Noah, here used as a metaphor for the cleansing trials of repentance.</note> of repentance—that is, with all kinds of crosses and tribulations—and destroys the animalistic, high-climbing word and the false light of confusion within man, and kills the harlot through the firmament <note>original: "Veste." Referring to the descendants of Esau.</note>, a triple harlot-like and literal **Babel of Sects**, which was full of confusion.

<meta>The previous page likely discussed the spiritual anatomy of man or the "Tree of Man," as indicated by the header. The catchword "er" (he) suggests a continuation of a sentence regarding the process of spiritual enlightenment.</meta>

# The Tree of Man.

The Spirit plays here somewhat behind the veil of Moses <note>A reference to 2 Corinthians 3, suggesting that the true spiritual meaning is hidden behind the literal text of the law</note>. One must lift the curtain and do away with the veil: then one will see how things stand at the beginning and the end of the Old and the New Covenant. For Babel <note>Babylon, representing spiritual confusion and worldly pride</note> exists right at the beginning of the Jewish Kingdom and of Christendom: and it is also the middle; where it was then built up quite high according to the letter: but Esau is the end. For just as Nimrod built Babel in the beginning: so Esau stands at the end and builds Babel, and sets up the first literal image again, so that the end may thus return to its beginning before it is rooted out and destroyed.

When we go even deeper to the foundation, this threefold image of Babel takes its beginning in the **Dragon**, its **middle** in the **Beast**, and its **end** in the **false prophet** <note>References to the Book of Revelation</note>. And every **human being** who was once specially enlightened—like Adam—and has become dark again through the Fall, is such a threefold Babel, image, and essence, for as long as he lives. Namely: a coarse-beastly image, which is lustful and alive in the astral realm of the seed; a feminine image, which is sensory in the Reason; and a masculine image, which is to be called the source-spirit <term>Quell-Geist: a term from Jacob Böhme's theosophy referring to the active, bubbling forces of nature or spirit</term> in the heart and the highest life of nature. # the Twelve Gates
