# Of the Fleshly Jerusalem

The spirit is the light of the outer body, a lamp upon the path, a light in the external world that assists man in all kinds of arts and works within the natural animal-life. See, dear soul: when the outer eye is seeing, it enlightens the whole man as the fleshly tabernacle <note>original: "Stiffts-Hütte," referring to the body as a temporary dwelling or tent for the soul, similar to the biblical Tabernacle</note>; it shines for him in his conduct, wherever he goes. And see further: when an eye is blind, it makes the whole body dark. A blind person, who must lack the light, is a very despised, poor, and miserable person; for he can serve neither himself nor his neighbor. On the other hand, he who has the light can do what he will: he makes his distinction in all kinds of work and can see what is good and pleasant in the near and far, and his light shines for him so that he can learn, read, and write something in letters, and also otherwise see to all kinds of useful labor. He can rejoice because he sees the sun, which gives light and life to all of nature.

But woe to him who has an evil eye <note>original: "Schalcks-Auge," implying a deceitful, malicious, or squinting eye that looks toward sin</note>; who walks in darkness despite the bright light and misuses his eyes; who is blind at midday so that his eyes slumber in the darkness; who makes night out of day and day out of night, like the irrational animals that go along without understanding and look after fleshly lusts! Woe to the high and haughty eye-spirits of Babylon! It will go badly for them, that they so misuse their eyes and look upon fleshly things: where one looks after beautiful clothes, the other after beautiful maidens; as it stands written: **Their eyes looked toward the daughters of men, how beautiful they were, and they fathered children, and from this came powerful tyrants.** <note>A reference to Genesis 6:2-4 regarding the Nephilim and the corruption of the world before the Flood.</note> Woe to them! # The Second Gate
## Of the Babel-Jerusalem within Man.

In the second Royal Eye-Gate dwells the Prince of Memory, acting as the Spirit of Reflection and Examination; he is a discerning prince in the distinction of what is seen. If he is divinely enlightened, then he understands the divine mystery: but if he is still blind in the distinction between evil and good, then he is in darkness and understands nothing; for he is filled with evil lust. If the Prince of Understanding is dark, then he is living-dead in sins, able to grasp or understand nothing, like a dead man: for he does not examine what may be good; and thus lives without discernment, like one deceased. He performs evil with pleasure, and the good remains hidden and locked away from him, just as one locks a gate. For the living foundation from within is not revealed to him; instead, he turns toward the outward world with his evil brother, the other Prince. Therefore, he is like one sleeping: when one sleeps, he knows of nothing, and the light remains hidden from him as long as he slumbers. Thus the Prince of Memory slumbers and sleeps, and is dead-blind through transgression and misdeed, more so than an untimely birth <note>original: "unzeitige Geburt," referring to a stillborn child or miscarriage</note> that has its grave within the mother's womb; for he makes no distinction between light and darkness. # Of the Carnal Jerusalem

## The Third Gate of Babylon-Jerusalem in Man.

In the third royal **Gate of the Nose** dwells the Prince of Smell.

Because man, having fallen away from **God**, has become animalistic and beast-like through the Fall <note>The "Fall of Man" refers to the biblical narrative of original sin in Eden</note>; I can represent this Babylonian Prince of Smell in the external man no better than through the animals. Therefore, look at a filthy pig, and see what a sharp spirit of smell dwells within it! It smells its food from afar with its nose, and runs about, and searches, until it has found what it seeks. A pig has great power in its nose: with the nose it smells and roots about, and tears up great fields and forests so that it may seek its food. And thus the Prince of the Nose is a true spirit of nourishment, lustful and dainty in the nature of creatures. It is even more notable in a small beetle, how it flies after a scent and seeks dung. And when it has flown a long way and found the same, it is so greedy that it crawls entirely inside and roots through it until the Prince of its Smell has sucked all the power into itself. Furthermore, one may look at a tracking-hound, how the animal-prince of its smell is such a mighty spirit that they can seek out animals and men in the most hidden corners, which the Prince of Smell tells them. A black raven and eagle smell carrion from a distance.

If such a mighty spirit of smell dwells in the animals, how much more must there be a much higher spirit in a human being? A Prince who certainly has great power, according to how he can awaken carnal lust <note>original: "Wollust"; implies both physical pleasure and spiritual desire</note>.

The spirit of smell is a nourishing-spirit <note>German: Nähr-Geist; a vitalist concept where the "spirit" or essence of a substance feeds the soul</note>, through which all other spirits and powers are strengthened and nourished. Through smell, the air is a medicine for health to man, and also a poison and pestilence unto death. When a person becomes faint and ill, he can be so refreshed and comforted by a fragrant water and restorative balsam <note>German: Schlag-Balsam; a traditional medicinal salve used against strokes or fainting spells</note> that he becomes alive again in all his spirits.

See, my beloved! so powerful is the Prince of Smell in the Gate of the Nose. Through this spirit, man can learn to test and distinguish everything in nature; and much more, when he is spiritually reborn, a man can test and make a distinction between good and evil, what is of God, of Christ, and of Belial <note>A biblical term for the personification of worthlessness or the devil</note>; as it is written:

> He shall smell through the Prince of Smell in the fear of the Lord. <note>A mystical paraphrase of Isaiah 11:3, "And shall make him of quick understanding in the fear of the Lord," where the original Hebrew implies "breathing" or "smelling" the fear of God</note>

## The Fourth Gate
### Of the Babel-Jerusalem in Man.

In the fourth royal Nose-Gate <note>The "Nose-Gate" refers to the sense of smell and the breath as one of the mystical entrances to the human soul.</note> dwells the puffing Prince of the Air, who is a mighty prince in both beast and man; indeed, a very great life-spirit in the soul, who may well be called a prince according to his high office, because of the very great power he holds in beasts and men. Upon this spirit hangs the entire life of the soul. In the pious, he is a light of the **Lord**, which, as often as it goes out and in <note>Referring to the rhythm of breathing.</note>, is impregnated by God’s light, so that it becomes full of dew and life-powers to strengthen all powers of the body and the soul. For thus man becomes a living soul through the breathing-upon of God, when he believes in **God** magically—that is, effectively <note>original: "würcksam"; the author defines "magical" not as sorcery, but as the active, working power of faith.</note>. Whenever the soul draws breath, either outward or inward, with the lifting of its mind <note>German: Gemüth; refers to the seat of emotions and soul-intent</note> toward God, it is continually strengthened and renewed in the spirit of light. For God the Lord, who is sun and light, has entirely possessed its heart; and the active power of the "Blower" <note>The "Blower" refers to the spiritual breath or the Holy Spirit acting upon the soul</note> always imagines <note>term: imagines/imaginiret. In Boehmean philosophy, this refers to the creative power of the mind to form a spiritual reality</note> into this light that is within its heart. God meets the soul everywhere: He is the light of its heart, and He is the light of the world: He speaks to it from without, and He speaks to it from within. If its magical spirit blows into God with heartfelt sighing, then God blows back into its spirit and accepts its sighing, filling it with light and wisdom, and grants to its working of faith whatever it has desired. 

There is a great difference between one who seeks, loves, and honors God, and one who does not desire Him. A person who does not actively seek God with all the powers of faith is like an irrational beast, and even much worse. An active, blowing spirit in an evil person is nothing but a fire-blower. Just as the bellows blow upon coals to create a glow, so an active animal-spirit <note>term: animal-spirit/Thier-Geist. Refers to the lower, instinctive nature of man unrefined by the divine</note> blows from within the person upon the dragon that dwells in the air and in his vice-filled heart. He is the bellows, and his evil thoughts, lusts, and desires of the flesh are fiery coals. These he blows up, and takes pleasure in dwelling within them with his active spirit. Then the dragon blows into it from without and within, so that they burn together in a bright blaze; and that is then a hellish fire. Now, the more the active spirit of the air <note>original: Lufft-Geist</note> in man imagines and blows into its fire, the more the fire burns, until the entire nature of the animal-man stands in full flame. # Concerning the Carnal Jerusalem

...ly-minded people are perverted to the highest degree. Consider this: every addiction to pleasure <note>original: "Lust-Sucht"</note> within you is a meal for the Devil! If you eat of it, then you are a person cursed before God! And the more you eat, the more you heap up hellish fire for yourself, in which you shall burn for eternity.

An irrational beast is not as evil as an irrational, carnally-minded human, because the spirit of the beast is entirely dark, being of the earth. In contrast, the human spirit is composed of light and darkness simultaneously. It does not surprise me that a lion, bear, wolf, dog, or dragon is evil; nor that a fox is cunning, a cat is false, a peacock or horse is proud, a <term>basilisk</term> <note>A mythical reptile said to kill with its gaze; used here to represent venomous nature.</note> is poisonous, a snake is crafty, or that a turkey-cock <note>original: "welscher Hahn"</note> turns fiery red when he is angry. But regarding a human, I marvel that he is so corrupt and possesses the qualities of all these aforementioned animals alone; especially since man is so very close to the Spirit of Light. Just as the moon is near the sun and is ignited by it, so can man be ignited by God and receive the light from Him. For just as the moon is both light and darkness, so also is man. And just as the earth is a dark <term>body</term> <note>original Latin: "Corpus"</note> and was created upon the deep, dark abyss, so also are the animals darkness and gloom, having been created from the dark element of the earth. Although they have very little light, being essentially created out of the darkness, there are nonetheless many animals that surpass man in their diligence to praise God; the nightingale and the lark, and others like them, are far above man in their diligence. Oh, what a heavy judgment will pass over the human who has rejected and despised the God of Lights, who followed after him all the days of his life! God has [subjected] the creature to...

# Concerning the Carnal Jerusalem

Firstly, it is to be noted that this princely spirit is a mighty spirit of life <note>German: *Lebens-Geist*; refers to the vital force or animating spirit within a human being</note>, without which spirit man is deaf, and entirely dark-stupid and stubborn. Just as one is blind without eyes, speechless without a mouth, and dead without a spirit: so too is man deaf without this princely spirit of life. But if someone possesses the spirit of hearing, then he can hear everything with open ears, as if he saw it with his eyes: and that is a great gift of God. Through this princely spirit of life, man must perceive everything from the outside. For it leads into understanding <note>German: *Verständniß*; the faculty of comprehension</note> that which is said and spoken from without. I must say that he is like a deaf adder and serpent, which stop their ears against the spirit that conjures them, so that they do not hear the voice of the conjurer <note>A reference to Psalm 58:4-5 regarding those who refuse to hear divine truth</note>, as the Spirit testifies, who well knows how to conjure; or else like a certain conscienceless beast, which flees from man because of its disobedience when it hears his voice, and this because it does not wish to be subject to man. And thus does the man who has fallen away from God. When God calls to him, from within or from without, he runs away like a wild animal, and growls like a growling bear and a snorting pig, which often turns and tears apart the one who calls to it; they bare their teeth like wolves and dogs; they spit in the face like false cats; they stop their ears like adders before the voice of the caller, the faithful servants of God, before God and the spirit of Jesus Christ. # The Seventh Gate of Babel-Jerusalem within Man.

In the seventh royal gate of the mouth dwells the princely spirit of eloquent speech within the tongue.

Just as man is now evil here as well following the Fall <note>Referring to the biblical Fall of Man, which the author believes corrupted human nature</note>, so too is this spirit evil. For the spirit of the mouth among men who possess animalistic qualities is a very harmful spirit of vice—more so than all others are. For this spirit has the great power to bring evil to light, and it is the spirit which all others must employ. For out of the perverse mouth of Babel <note>Babel here represents the state of confusion and sin, contrasted with the holy Jerusalem</note> proceed blasphemy, jesting, ribaldry, buffoonery, cursing, swearing, lying, shaming, and more of the like. This spirit does not only defile itself, but it is also very harmful and dangerous to others alongside it. The spirit of the mouth is more poisonous than an adder's tongue, more fierce than a bear or a lion. I would rather fight with scorpions than have to do with false tongues; and I would rather be spat upon by a basilisk <note>A mythical reptile said to kill with its breath or gaze</note> than hear the praise and blasphemy of a poisoned tongue. When I hear a slanderer and a blasphemer, it feels to me no different than if I saw the abyss smoking and hell standing open. Their throat is like an open grave, standing full of stinking dead corpses and rotting bodies. Every single word in their mouth and upon their tongue is a stinking carrion: whoever eats of it becomes mad and foolish; and he whose food it becomes is a beast of prey, more than a mad dog, raven, eagle, wolf, bear, or lion, which otherwise behave as

## The Eighth Gate
### Of the Babel-Jerusalem within Man.

The eighth royal Well-Gate or Milk-Gate of the breasts, which issues forth from the fountain of the soul-breasts, has been signified through the outer opening of the breasts of the body. Over this Milk-Gate is placed the welling Priest-Prince, who waters, nourishes, and sustains the soul-powers with the drink of all kinds of instruction and teachings.

Now, if this angelic Priest-Prince of souls is reborn, then his teaching is like milk and good wine in the <term>cluster of camphire</term><note>original: "Trauben Copher," a reference to Song of Solomon 1:14, symbolizing a fragrant and precious gift</note> of the soul-breasts, which flows out as pure as milk and clear wine to water all souls in the <term>microcosm</term><note>original: "kleinen Welt," referring to the human being as a miniature reflection of the universe</note> with the drink of life. These souls are the spiritual powers, being the offspring and children of the great soul, of which she begets as many within herself as there are drops of dew in the <term>dawn</term><note>original: "Morgenröthe," a common mystical term for the beginning of divine enlightenment</note>: and the entire great soul of man is their mother, in whom they dwell, walk, gather together, and worship. # The Ninth Gate
## Of the Babel-Jerusalem within Man.

The ninth royal gate of the second breast is the Food-Gate <note>original: "Speiß-Thor"</note>, which goes out from the soul; this gate is marked by the external opening of the breasts.

Over this gate, the priestly Prince of Food and Nourishment is appointed as the Master of Provisions.

Now, if this firstborn son of the soul is reborn, he serves the gentle Lamb of God as a spiritual king and priest of the powers. But if he is un-reborn, he serves the spirit of the stars <note>original: "Geiſt des Geſtirns," referring to the astrological influences on the physical body</note>, as the impure stellar-regime in its out-flowings. The foods that he gives to his offspring are the fruits of the forbidden tree, namely the Tree of Lusts. The fruits thereon are all kinds of delights to be eaten according to the flesh, such as the fruit of evil activity, and so forth. The Prince himself is the supreme soul-power of powers. Just as here on earth all men are like one another; yet there are some among them who [rule] over the whole body of...

These branches are nothing other than the very widely spread government of vice, under which he governs and holds them captive as prisoners and slaves.

# The Eleventh Gate
## Of the Babel-Jerusalem within Man. <note>The author uses "Babel-Jerusalem" to describe the dual nature of the human condition: the confusion of the fallen state (Babel) and the potential for divine restoration (Jerusalem).</note>

The eleventh royal gate of conception and birth is the seed-organ and the place of birth in the woman, wherein the angelic priest-prince of impregnation dwells as a particular spirit in both the male and the female. This is the forming-spirit <note>original: "Formirungs-Geist"</note>, who prepares that which is to be sown and born: namely, on the male side, the seed that is to be received; and on the female side, the birth: and he also prepares the way for both. However, I do not wish to speak here of fleshly matters, but of spiritual things, as I do in all places. For I only present the outward things here as a symbolic model.

The soul, then, which is thus impregnated, is our life or life-fire, which always burns and is ignited in delight, and is feminine. # The Twelfth Gate
## Of the Babel-Jerusalem within Man.

The twelfth gate is the Dung-Gate or Cleansing-Gate / wherein dwells the Prince of Cleansing and Leading-Out / who cleanses man and scours him again from his inward filth and foulness. <note>The author refers to the Dung Gate of Nehemiah 3:14, interpreting it as a spiritual necessity for the soul to expel its "waste."</note>

However, it is not my intention here to describe the outward nature of the body with its filth / but rather the spiritual soul-man with his filth and foulness / and how he must be scoured again from the raw matter <note>original: "Rauh"</note> which he has swallowed. And that can happen in no other way than through the single Word: **Change your mind!** <note>original: "Aendert euren Sinn!" - a translation of the Greek *metanoia*, often rendered as "repentance."</note> That is to say / Spit out the foulness with which you have corrupted your soul! For when the old man / who lives according to nature / has fulfilled the number of the twelfth birth / he must either be broken / or changed and transformed into a spiritual, God-governed being. As we then have sufficient examples of this in the kingdom of nature / and especially after the Deluge in Ham / the son of Noah / who was an arch-villain and godless man / so that he inherited the curse from his father Noah instead of the blessing / and especially in his youngest son Canaan / as it is written: **Cursed be Canaan!** and so forth.

This Ham is regarded here by the Spirit of God as a carnal and dark soul-man, or as a particular world upon earth: just as the three sons of Noah prefigure three distinct worlds that were to come upon the earth in the future; namely, first, Ham the carnal world; Japhet the God-lawful world <note>A world governed by divine law or religious morality</note>; and Shem the paradisiacal world. Now Ham, as the dark soul-world, began to beget the first child of sin and vice, Canaan, over which vice the curse was spoken while it was still in the mother's womb. Then this vice begat eleven more princes of vice after him; so that Canaan, their father, the prince of the first vice, was the twelfth in Ham. And this was thus the curse that went out from Noah in Ham over this twelfth birth, which had made the "old man" <note>A biblical term for the unregenerate, sinful nature of humanity</note> perfect in the measure of sin, as can be read in Genesis 9:25 and Chapter 10:15-19. When the time of the "man of sin" in Canaan was over in the carnal world, and he had filled the measure of sin, the God-lawful man came in his place as the Seed of Israel, and broke the dominion and kingdom of the carnal man Canaan, and rooted out the members of sin, of which there was such a mighty multitude at that time on earth in the land of Canaan, all of whom were Ham's children.

Do you see here how sin, when it increases within a person, creates a great sea full of births in the soul, so that it is almost impossible to root out, unless God brings forth a God-lawful, zealous seed of righteousness against sin, and attacks it with sharpness, and leaves not a single one unpunished, but [becomes] master over...

The serpent of these two <note>referring to the dual nature of the internal struggle</note> is the astral desire of the flesh—the reason and seed of the flesh—which often entices the Jerusalem of God's Law, *Hevah* <note>Eve, representing the sensory, will-driven soul</note>, the holy-willed sensory Soul of God within the understanding, to lust against the Lord and to eat her fruit of pleasure from the paradise of the flesh. This fruit she daily plucks from the tree of malice, security, falsehood, deceit, lustfulness, suspiciousness, lewdness, gluttony, drunkenness, and other cravings, sin, and shame. She then offers it to *Hevah*, the small light of the understanding and will within the memory, so that she might consent through her counsel to eat of this lustfulness against the command and prohibition of GOD. Now, if this God-legal soul, *Hevah*, is still weak, she allows herself to be persuaded and captivated by the serpent of the malice of her flesh and the desires of reason; she eats what the serpent of reason and flesh offers her, in that the serpent—the fleshly essence of the external man who has gained dominion—takes the first bite from the outside. Afterward, the desire and pleasure to eat also reaches *Hevah* in the will and the God-legal senses; thus she takes the second bite, in that she has allowed the weak will-understanding of the upper senses to be overcome by the serpent. And then the inner will-spirit of the heart is also weakened, namely *Adam* <note>representing the inner spirit or higher mind</note>, after the sensory will-soul in the upper understanding has fallen away and surrendered itself to the serpent—the external animal part—whereby she also pulls the inner part down after her.

# Concerning the Jerusalem of the Law

<note>The author uses "Jerusalem of the Law" to refer to a state of spiritual discipline and legal righteousness required after the Fall.</note>

...seduced the earthly Adam and drew him after her: so that Wisdom had to retreat when she saw that the inner man of the heart—the great will-spirit of the understanding—also turned outward following the will of Eve <note>original: "Hevah"</note> into the upper parts of the brain; for he took the apple from the hand of Eve and ate. Thus Adam, the source-sun in the heart, was struck; as was the moon, Eve, in the understanding of the upper senses; and the nature of the outward man: for one seduced the other, and made them fall away from God.

O Eve and Adam! You two wills of the upper and inner understanding! What have you done? Were you not made masters over your wild beasts upon the whole earth? (Genesis 1:28, 3:19) Namely, over the animal serpents and the stellar powers <term>stellar powers: the astrological or cosmic influences believed to govern the physical body</term> of the flesh of the outer world, which were within your outer man. How does it happen then, that the inward Adam-like source-spirit of the heart is now a servant; and the woman, the upper spirit of sense and will, is a handmaid? And that which was formerly the servant—the serpent and the stellar power of the animal world—is now the master and a dragon, possessing the heart in Adam’s stead? And the world of the flesh of the outer man and the power of sensual reason in the flesh, which was formerly the handmaid, has now become the mistress, and now holds dominion over the whole man along with the spirit that was formerly the servant? Oh! Who will deliver us from the body of sin and death? <note>A reference to Romans 7:24</note> God be heartily thanked, that he has sent us his dear Son, the new Adam, for redemption! Who now fights within us against the old serpent of lust, and through the discipline of God's law drives it out of us again, and willingly and gladly suffers its "heel-sting" <note>A reference to the prophecy in Genesis 3:15</note> within us.

suffers until he has cast them out of us again and swept the heart with the broom of repentance <note>German: "Buß-Besem"; a metaphor for the rigorous internal cleansing required in Pietist spirituality</note>; so that he may dwell within as the second, new-Adamic source-spirit and Lord of all depths and heights and widths and breadths of all natures that have been purified through him.

Now, whereas in the preceding description I have dealt with the dark Heathen or Babylon-Serpent-Jerusalem and its twelve gates and twelve unclean angelic princes—representing the murderer at the left hand of Christ—I will now deal with the God-ordained Legal Jerusalem of the murderer and repentant thief on the cross at the right hand of Christ. Here, Christ, the new Adam, has stepped into the middle between the two as the Mediator and the God-ordained zeal of repentance; he who now stands between darkness and light, and separates the darkness from the light, and becomes the light himself that divides the day and the night from one another—namely, the evil from the good—for which he is harshly crucified by the great darkness on the left hand and the sins of the world, for which he atones on the cross.

# and its 12 Gates.

These, then, are the twelve gates of the God-ordained Jerusalem of the sinner and the thief at the right hand of **Jesus Christ**; he who turned away from the blasphemous serpent at the left hand of **Jesus Christ**, and turns back with a repentant heart toward the new Adam, **Jesus Christ**, the Cross-Tree of Life in the center, as the supreme Paradisiacal Prince of Life and the inner fountain-spring of the heart. As soon as he confessed his sin from the heart, saying:

<margin>he.</margin>
> The new man Christ has done nothing improper; while he rebuked the blasphemous spirit on the left: But we, as murderers of the birthright and the light, and as rebels, suffer justly what our deeds deserve;

<margin>he.</margin>
he spoke further, as one now condemned to death on the cross and as a repentant sinner begging **God** for forgiveness:

> **Lord**, remember me when you have torn asunder the darkness-chains of hell and of death, and have come into your kingdom!

Then the most holy Prince of the Evangelical Paradise <note>The "Evangelical Paradise" refers here to the state of grace promised in the Gospel, contrasted with the "Wrath-Law" of the Old Testament.</note> answered the condemned sinner of the strict Law of Wrath above him:

> Truly, I say to you: Today, if you continue thus with your zeal for repentance, you shall also overcome, and be with me in the God-ordained Paradise! And there you shall learn to know me again as the Tree of Life, and I will give you to eat from the wood of life, and gradually draw you back to myself.

<margin>he.</margin>
Your outer man must remain above on the cross through the strict Law of Wrath of the justice of **God**, and suffer punishment for his deeds; the old man <note>The "old man" is a Pauline term for the unregenerate, sinful nature of humanity.</note> must be entirely broken on the cross because he has offended **God**. But from within, I will teach you through the staff of the Law of Discipline of my mouth, and turn and lead you

# Concerning the Law-Jerusalem

The Prince of Lewdness, the Prince of Lovelessness, the Prince of Avarice, the Prince of Malice and tyrannical wickedness, the Prince of the carnal mind, the Prince of the false light of reason, and the Prince of Manslaughter. These are twelve great Princes of Vice and Sin, who even now have just as many colonels and captains sitting beside them upon their thrones, who rule within the soul; together with the twelve first-born <note>The "first-born" here refers to the primary or original impulses of sin that dominate the unregenerate soul.</note>, they govern over the darkness and the evil cravings and desires of the flesh, and over the dark senses of reason, driving them toward sin. Whatever the Prince—the first-born among the indwelling sins—commands, the subjects must carry out into action; this continues until the Prince of all Purities is finally admitted through the soul that is subject to God's law. He then brings the divine-legal whip of discipline and strikes out at them with the Law of the Ten Words <note>The Ten Commandments.</note>:

> You shall not kill! You shall not steal! You shall not misuse the name of your Lord! You shall not covet! and: Cursed be everyone who does not abide by all things written in the Law! <note>original: "Maledictus omnis qui non permanet in omnibus quae scripta sunt in libro legis." This is a reference to Galatians 3:10 / Deuteronomy 27:26.</note>

When He thus thunders behind them with the staff of the Law, then repentance begins to arrive with a great anguish of heart within the temple of the soul that is subject to God's law; the soul then humbles itself and moderates the impure cravings of its lustful desires. And so, the twelve first-born of impurities begin to grow fearful and anxious: for they perceive that their time to depart has now come, because the Light is now to be received; and they also know who He is to whom they must yield—namely, the Son and Holy One of God.

<summary>This page describes the internal spiritual battle where twelve personified "Princes of Vice" rule the soul until the "Prince of Purities" arrives to enforce the Ten Commandments. The arrival of divine law triggers repentance and the eventual expulsion of these sinful impulses to make room for the Son of God.</summary>

### Second Expulsion
# of the Prince of Impurity
## at the Gate of Birth.

Now that the Spirit and Princely Angel of Impregnation <note>The author uses "impregnation" here in a spiritual sense, referring to the divine seed of grace being planted in the soul.</note> has entered through the Gate of Birth with his Divine Radiance, he has struck the Prince of Impurity <note>original: "Unkeuschheit," referring to unchastity or moral lewdness.</note> hard and taken him captive. Thus, he has occupied the soul at this location of the Gate of Birth with a God-ordained power, which now begins to grow and sprout within the soul like a small seed-grain of God. Consequently, the soul begins to become pregnant with a pure, angelic desire for doing good, and begins to honor and love the Holy Law as its schoolmaster <note>original: "Zucht-Meister," a disciplinarian or tutor, likely referencing Galatians 3:24.</note>—the very law that subdued and impregnated it by force. The soul now carries a feminine, yet still weak, birth of Light, which could easily be killed again if it were not suckled, fed, and nourished by God within the "womb" until it grows into the greater powers of the Light.

It is to be noted, however, that every birth of these twelve Firstborn Princes carries the twelve powers of birth within itself when it enters and takes up its post in the soul through the Law of God. Thus, this image is now a little daughter of twenty-four powers of Light, strong in its conception. These were now two Princes who have stationed themselves within the soul: the Prince of Purity over the Gate of Waste-Excretion <note>original: "Mist-Ausführungs-Thor," referring to the spiritual purging of "filth" or sin.</note> and the Prince of Impregnation over the Gate of Birth of the soul.

# On the Jerusalem of the Law

## The Third Casting Out
# of the Prince of Lustfulness
### above the Navel Gate.

Now that the two princes of God's law have occupied the two lowest gates, and the soul—as the City of God—has henceforth become pregnant through the Holy Spirit; the third power now begins to be conceived, namely the princely power of nourishment <note>original: "Säugung," literally suckling or nursing</note> above the Navel Gate. This power begins to combat the lustful desires within the soul and casts them out, so that the seed of the Eternal Father—the Light and Word of Life—may gain better room to spread itself within the soul. 

In such a manner, the soul increasingly progresses in the growth of the Offering of God that has been conceived within her. This occurs because several virtues are now beginning to be conceived within her, with which she goes pregnant; for now the third of the twelve princes of light according to the Law of God begins, and has already begun, to station himself at the Navel Gate. He is like a goblet that never lacks for drink <note>A reference to Song of Solomon 7:2, often interpreted mystically as a source of spiritual refreshment</note> for the nourishment of the still weak, yet steadily growing, Little Child of Light, which now increases in age and virtues within the grace of God. 

The soul, however, now begins to enter into great birth-pangs with her fruit, suffering much from both without and within. The birth—the sacrificial Lamb of God's law—must also endure much along with her, because it is now beginning to receive life, to live holily, and Christ is beginning to take on a form within the soul.

### Fourth Casting Out
# of the Prince of Gluttony and Drunkenness
### of Impurity
## at the Left Breast-Gate.

Now that the first three of the twelve princes and elders have been received <note>This refers to the spiritual virtues replacing the vices</note>, and the Soul-City of the God-ordained Jerusalem begins to receive holiness and virtues; the left breast-gate is now stormed for the fourth time. The Prince of Gluttony and Drunkenness is besieged and finally cast out, having already lost his best reinforcements; he was forced to retreat, heavily beaten by the increasing Birth of Light <note>A mystical term for the growth of Christ or divine wisdom within the soul</note>. Thus, the Prince of Nourishment <note>The virtuous counterpart to gluttony</note> now enters and begins to powerfully occupy the God-ordained soul with powers of light. As a result, the soul proceeds well in her pregnancy, and the child being born within her already learns to endure strong food. The Dragon <note>A reference to the Devil or the lower nature, as seen in the Book of Revelation</note>, however, begins to rage as he sees that four gates have already been taken; he makes the God-ordained soul very anxious and fearful, seeking to devour her little child—but in vain.

# Concerning the Jerusalem of the Law.

The seventh power-birth of the Woman. Now the Boy is a youth under God’s law, who increases from day to day in age, wisdom, and grace before **God** and mankind, because the wellspring of the Father has now been revealed to him from within. He now hears and follows the Father's voice, and he teaches the soul, Jerusalem, his mother, who bore him with pain and has raised him until now. ### Eighth Casting Out
# of the Prince of Greed
#### and of Withholding
## above the Gate of the Right Ear.

After he has struggled there for a long time against the prince of insatiable greed in willing, knowing, and hearing, he has finally overcome him along with all those who evilly observe **God**. He has rooted out the "much-hearing" of greed <note>The term "much-hearing" (Viel-hören) refers to a distracted or acquisitive way of listening that seeks to possess knowledge rather than be transformed by it.</note> in the gate of evil observation and the withholding of that which is from **God**. Thus, the eighth power of **God** is now born in the soul, so that she can be attentive in **God** and keep in her heart everything that this Youth—the Lamb of God’s law—tells her into the right ear of attentiveness.

# Of the New Jerusalem.

Thus, the outer humanity of Christ—which, through divine imitation and a shared mind with Him, also becomes our own humanity—is a wall around the inner city of the soul, Jerusalem. And in this human, outer, holy nature, acting as the soul's wall, the names of the twelve apostles of the Lamb shall now appear, established upon twelve foundations. This is so that the inner holy Jerusalem of the soul may now also possess a holy wall—that is, a holy outer humanity, like an outer city of Jerusalem—which follows the Lamb wherever He goes, just as the inner soul had to do previously. Yet the inner soul could not bring this to fruition without the outer, nor can the outer now function without the inner.

Therefore, the twelve gates and the angels upon them now appear, along with the twelve foundations and the twelve names of the twelve apostles of the Lamb, involving both the spiritual and physical Jerusalem at once. These two together bring about the completion <note>The "completion" or "perfection" refers to the mystical union of body and spirit in the believer.</note> by simultaneously attacking the inner and outer enemies in body and soul, killing them and offering them as a sacrifice.

These are the twelve gates of the outer and the twelve gates of the inner Jerusalem and their holy city wall together, alongside the twelve angels and the names of the apostles of the Lamb and of the children of Israel:

I. **The outer and inner firstbirth above the gate of the left eye is the far-seeing light of the holy souls: together with their holy life and conduct on the outside, wherein the disciples and followers of the Lamb shall appear as lights of the world.**

And the entire holy nature-soul of GOD was nothing but a gentle fire : and this holy fire-world was the four great depths. # Of the Dark World

Nor can it <note>referring to the fallen creation or nature</note> be helped back to its rightful state until, first and foremost, this Adamic world is restored. But when we are awakened from the dead, then they shall also be brought back through us; just as Christ already made the beginning of this after his departure. Therefore, let us begin to serve God from the heart, and be no longer disobedient, but obedient to Him, so that help may come again to us and to them!

Now we wish to contemplate and thoroughly examine the entirely dead and eternally cursed and rejected Adam-Lucifer <note>The author conflates the fall of the first man, Adam, with the fall of the angel Lucifer to show their shared rebellious nature</note> in the sea of the lowest abyss of our corrupted natural birth: partly as he is of and in himself, and partly as he is found within our unregenerate man of the flesh.

Behold the sun in the perverted, dark heart—how dim it has become! In the beginning, this was the beautiful angel Lucifer, as the first-born Adam in the deep world. God did not create him so dark; rather, he tore himself loose from the Law of God, turned away from God, and fell backward. Therefore, through his disobedience, he robbed himself of the Light of God and of the agonizing <note>original: "quallirenden" - likely referring to the 'Qual' or 'source-quality' of the divine spirit in Behmenist terminology</note> Spirit of God. For he, with his world and his angels, became Satan, who sought to annihilate the Word of Creation through which they were made, and presumed to dispute the created world and to storm the Throne of God. But God fortified His Throne-World with a great gulf. As the angelic princes in the Gates of Death then fought with the angels in the Gates of Life, God cut...

# Concerning the Dark World.

His birthing Word—which is the Light—withdrew and withheld itself entirely from them. Although the Light descends to them from the starry circles of God’s holy Throne- and Crown-World <note>The author refers to the highest celestial spheres of the divine realm</note> and struggles against them, it nevertheless remains hidden from them. It is just as the light was withdrawn from the Egyptians for three days, so that they had to sit in darkness and the shadow of death; yet Israel had nothing but pure light in and around them, even in the midst of the darkness: for God was their light even in the midst of Hell. This is a great comfort to the children of God, that the light shines for them in the dark and shows the way they should walk. But the darkness does not see it and does not know it, even though it is in and around them. For just as fire and light lie hidden within stones, so too does the light lie hidden within such dark spirits. But they do not wish to enter into suffering so that the fire and light might be struck out of them <note>A metaphor for spiritual refinement through hardship, like striking flint to create a spark</note>, and they do not wish to work within God, but only within the dark Devil and the constellations: therefore they are imprisoned in the night and in sins, and are shackled by them as if with chains and bonds. And that is what is written there:

> **The light shines in the darkness; but the darkness**

For every depth was created with its star-kingdom in such a way that it should give birth to God within itself: and God is the Light. 

But because the Luciferian <term>Adam of the Law</term><note>Gesetzes-Adam; a reference to the fallen state of man or angelic beings bound by legalism rather than grace</note> did not want to give birth to God and His Son within himself, his heart—that is, the center-point of the depth—became dark and gloomy, because no light shone for him anymore that Lucifer could have ignited. Therefore, the Luciferian solar body is now entirely dark. Inwardly, however, it is fiery; outwardly, there is a great smoke and vapor. 

But the fire is a harsh fire that eats away at itself, like fire in rotting wood that gives off such a terrible smoke: for the wrath of God burns within it. Therefore, he goes about like a roaring lion in the very center of the lowest abyss, tormenting and agonizing the souls who lie with him in this anguish and deathly torment, and who miserably languish in the smoke of their torment and in the fire of the terrible judgments of God. Not that God descended from heaven and tormented them; not at all! For God is Love. Rather, they must judge and condemn one another themselves, because they have rejected God, who is the only Love, and have followed Lucifer, the enemy of God's love, when he waged war against God; even though they were all [filled] with the light of God the Holy Spirit...

# Concerning the Dark World.

## How the perverted heart, turned away from God, along with the entire Abyssal Dragon-Beast and the false prophet, exists within every unregenerate person.

Thus, as previously mentioned, not only is Lucifer a dark devil because he did not remain in the truth—causing his world to become a dark dwelling—but the carnal man of sin, fallen away from God, is exactly such a carnal, dark, and dead soul-world. Drowned in all malice, he walks in a wild, raw condition and falls deeper and deeper into it. Whether he be a Christian, Jew, or Heathen, it is all the same for him. He is the dark, perverted heart that opposes God in all things. Indeed, he is an essential devil and Satan <note>original: "Satan," from the Hebrew for 'adversary'</note> from the world of the abyss, out of the depths below; as it is written: *You are from beneath*; and again: *You are of your father the devil!* <note>John 8:23 and 8:44</note> For he was a murderer of souls, and they too are murderers of both body and soul.

Now, when a person remains in this raw state, he is not one, but three. Namely: he sins with the body and all evil desires that are in the flesh, as in the external members of the body; and he sins with the sensual, rational soul <term>rational soul: the "Vernunffts-Seele," or the part of the human psyche governed by logic and worldly intellect rather than divine spirit</term>, wherein he devises all manner of evil schemes to sin; and he sins with the spirit, as he spews out all manner of abominable thoughts and blasphemies from his heart against God, his neighbor, and all the children of God. These three now are the rebellious company of Korah, Dathan, and Abiram <note>Numbers 16; biblical figures who led a rebellion against Moses and were swallowed by the earth</note>, who

# Of the Dark World.

This is the Abiram who has his throne in the unrepentant flesh, the brain, and the perverted dark heart; he is of the lineage of Ham <note>Noah's son, traditionally associated with the origin of cursed or worldly peoples</note>, who has always rebelled against the **Lord** and his Anointed, to fight against the law of God in Israel. **Nimrod**, the first god of reason <note>original: "Vernunffts-Gott" — referring to human intellect divorced from divine grace</note>, builds the fleshly Babel and the tower of reason of all godlessness. This god of the flesh, with his height in the heart, brain, and flesh, has now brought things so far that he can no longer be driven out. This is the great red dragon who possesses and has possessed the soul of the abyss; the law of sin and death, which leads man from one abomination to another, and from one sinful lust of the flesh to another. For the ten fleshly kings of the law of the abyss have been multiplied into ten times ten, and have uncovered the abyss in the three worlds: in the **Babylonian** world of confusion, in the **Egyptian** world of the flesh,

# Concerning the Dark World

world, and in the Greek dragon-world <note>original: "Griechischen Drachen-Welt"; likely a reference to the constellation Draco or the mythological chaos-monster representing the fallen astral realm</note>, so that the perverted, threefold man has become truly evil and godless against GOD and his neighbors.

Thus, the fourfold abyss of the deep is now also the unregenerate and unspiritual soul of man. Likewise, the twelve gates with the twelve-times-twelve star-princes <note>A reference to the 144,000 or the astrological governing powers</note> are also all around within the soul; these gates are also marked upon our external animal-bodies. Do you ask: Which are they? Answer: Count the openings of the head, and you will find seven. Continue and count the openings of the body; you will find five, including the navel. These are the signs of the internal soul-world. For in the soul, these twelve gates are essential.

Do you ask: What then is the soul? Answer: The soul is the composite spirit of life <term>Lebens-Geist: the vital force that animates the physical frame</term> in the whole human being, a fiery worm of the body's sensitivity, which dwells through the entire body as the living, moving, and sensing. And this soul has its growth from the powers of the food which has grown up with the human being from youth, through meat and drink. Do you not feel it, how it is like a devouring fire when it does not receive its accustomed food? And when it must suffer thirst, do you not feel how it writhes, groans, and longs for water or something else, so that it might quench its burning thirst? When you have a dispute with someone, do you not feel it in your chest and liver, and in your gall, how it is so violent against your adversary? If it often had the power, it would swallow and tear him apart alive.

# Of the Dark World.

Do you not see how fierce it is to execute judgment and to shed blood, among both the high and the low? When you are sick, do you not feel what pains it must suffer in your body? If there were no soul, the body would be dead and could not suffer. But because the soul exists, the body is suffering and sensitive through the soul. Thus the soul is the life, and the life is in the blood, which wanders through the whole human being every 24 hours, passing through the twelve gates of the soul. And in the blood is the constellation of the heavens <note>The author refers to the "astrum" or sidereal spirit, believing the macrocosm of the stars is mirrored within the human microcosm.</note>, which moves about with the blood. But the blood is animalistic: and the "animals" in the blood are the starry animal-powers <note>original: "thier-Kräfften"; likely referring to the zodiacal forces or biological instincts.</note>, which swim in the fourfold abyss of the blood.

The outer animalistic man has two arms and two feet, wherein he has his greatest bodily power and strength. And in these four abysses, the twelve strongest joints and limbs are created. And in these twelve limbs is the marrow, as the highest strength of these limbs. But on the outside is the blood: it runs through the twelve main joints, as through twelve strong gates that stand open to the indwelling soul-spirit of nature. Thus the starry soul in the blood never has any rest, but runs continually. But the strongest princes dwell in the marrow. These are the "garrison-spirits" <note>original: "Besatzungs-Geister"; a metaphor for the deep-seated vital forces or spiritual entities guarding the inner core of the body.</note> who dwell in their courts, and thus wall and entrench themselves round about so that they are not disturbed by external things.

## 1. The voice of the Lord is upon the waters!

This is the beginning of the new creation, when God intends to create Heaven and Earth out of the great, wide, and deep abyss of the sea: there God the Lord thunders with His Word of creation upon the great waters, with which He opens up the abysses.

## 2. The voice of the Lord is powerful!

This is now the emphasis, when God intends to create a firmament <note>original: "Veste," referring to the biblical firmament or expanse from Genesis 1:6</note> in the sea of the soul, between the lower and upper powers within the soul.

## 3. The voice of the Lord is full of majesty!

Now that a firmament has been created in the soul, the upper powers gather together like a stream into the sea of the soul; whereas before the soul was entirely weakened, and the evil powers were separated, just as one often separates clear and cloudy water from one another.

## 4. The voice of the Lord breaks the cedars; yes, the Lord breaks the cedars of Lebanon!

After the fourth voice had created grass and herbs and all kinds of fruitful trees, which were afterwards corrupted through the Fall into sin; now the fourth imagination <note>original: "Magination," a term used in Behmenist or mystical thought to describe the creative formative power of the mind or spirit</note> of God breaks what it had previously made with great power. These are the twelve-times-twelve princes in the twelve tribes of Babel <note>Babel here represents the confused, fallen state of worldly power and false religion</note>. These great trees must be broken, weakened, and—like the twelfth tree, Nebuchadnezzar, the King of Babel—hewn down.

## 5. The voice of the Lord strikes like flames of fire!

Now that God [has placed] many lights in the heavenly

The **Soul-Firmament** <note>original: *Seelen-Veste*; likely referring to the "expanse" created on the second day of Genesis, here applied to the inner spiritual sky of the believer</note> creates a great light to rule the day in the heart, and a lesser light to rule the night in the senses; this is the **fifth voice of God** in the darkness, consisting of pure flames of fire that ignite everything combustible all around, consuming all the strengths of the **old Adam** <note>A common theological term for the fallen, sinful nature of humanity inherited from Adam</note>.

### 6. The voice of the Lord stirs the wilderness!

After God created great whales and wild beasts in the sixth **Magination** <note>A technical term in Behmenist or Pietist mysticism referring to the creative power of the "imagination" or the formative spiritual process</note>, they are now violently stirred. Whereas they were accustomed to lying in the depths of the darkness, they must now see that the new **Word of Creation**—the sixth voice of God—drives them out from every place, so that they become anxious and fearful.

### 7. The seventh voice of the Lord stirs the wilderness of Kadesh.

*Kath* or *Koth* in our German tongue means "filth" or "muck." <note>The author uses a folk etymology, linking the biblical place name Kadesh to the German word *Kot* (excrement/mud) to emphasize spiritual impurity</note>. And by this may be understood the mucky human doctrines, or the three unclean frog-spirits or teaching-spirits following the sixth vial of wrath, which stir up the entire circle of the inner dark world to besiege the soul with all evil spirits in **Armageddon**—that is, in the **rational imagination** <note>original: *Vernunffts-Magination*</note> of the dragon, the beast, and the false prophet. As one can see in the copperplate engraving, the flames of fire emerge from the mouths of the dragon, the beast, and the false prophet, pursuing the Lamb. And thus the **Lamb** of God’s new creation must suffer much with the soul, which lies in hell, in the jaws of the devil, imprisoned and bound with many impurities and the cords and bonds of sin.

Adam was the first image of God, into whose heart God gave His holy Law, as He did to the holy Patriarchs after him. But Noah was the last, and he concludes exactly as the 2,000 years of the first world came to an end: and he was the tenth from Adam.

# Concerning the Legalistic World

The soul was subject to its lusts and desires throughout the whole land of Egypt <note>original: "Egypten-Land"; here used allegorically to represent the state of spiritual bondage or the material world</note>, and the twelve-fold Seed of God was held in great honor during the time while this pure zeal of Joseph lived. After his death, that zeal began to grow cold, for it had died with Joseph. Then the Devil of the Flesh returned to his reign—the angel from the abyss with an unchaste countenance. He violently oppressed the Seed of the Word of God through fleshly lusts, which grew up again within the dark souls of the abyss.

Now, when the Seed of God was sown so heavily among the thorns that it was thought to be entirely choked out, God awakened an excellent, fire-burning zeal named **Moses**. He set the thorns on fire so that they burned fiercely. And through this holy zeal—which possessed a shining face so bright that no fleshly Israelite could look upon him—God redeemed His entire people Israel. He punished the "Old Man" <note>A Pauline theological term referring to the unregenerate, sinful nature of humanity</note> through the discipline of the ten legalistic plagues of the land, and finally cast him into the Red Sea with his entire army. Thus the Seed of God was set free and became a great mountain, which no wild beast dared approach, nor any other animal touch.

However, after the Seed of God had increased beyond measure and continued to grow in the soul of Abraham, weeds began to enter in. The children of God fell into a false freedom and worshipped their fleshly "calf-lusts" <note>A reference to the Golden Calf, symbolizing the idolatry of material desires</note>. Then their good zeal hid itself and returned to God, who had sent it. Although they had shortly before heard the ten legalistic voices of thunder with their own ears speaking within them through the ministry of angels, they nevertheless allowed themselves to be led astray, until the first zeal returned and...

# Concerning the Legal World.

The devils sinned in Babylon <note>original: "Babel"; often used by this author to represent a state of spiritual confusion and worldly pride</note> / and in such a way that they might all the sooner come to the recognition of their sins / so that **GOD** might thus plow them anew / and sow His seed into them / which was entirely pure.

For then, during and after the captivity <note>referring to the Babylonian Captivity as an allegory for the soul's bondage to sin</note>, the zealots arise again in the Law / who are zealous against sin / and rebuild the holy City of the Soul / which they had previously laid waste through their godlessness / when they lived according to the flesh / and were forced to die. Namely, there was **Ezra** and **Nehemiah** / both of whom were excellent zealots: There was the high-priestly angel **Joshua** / who flew through heaven with the eternal Law of **GOD** : There was **Joachim** / **Eliashib** / **Joiada** / **Jonathan** / **Jaddua** / **Onias** / **Simon the Just** / **Eleazar** / **Manasseh** / **Onias** the Second / **Simon** the Second / **Onias** the Third / **Jason** / **Onias** the Fourth or **Menelaus** / **Lysimachus** / **Alcimus** or **Joachim** / **Judas Maccabeus** / whom none among all these zealots of the Law surpassed in the godly-legal soul of **Israel**. Forasmuch as he fought with the dragon **Antiochus** <note>Antiochus IV Epiphanes, here allegorized as the "dragon" of worldly lust</note> / that cruel desire for pleasure / and overcame him as a brave warrior / and was like **David** / and possessed a great clarity / like **Joshua** and **Caleb** / and a great power / to lead the senses and desires out of Babylon / so that they would no longer mingle with fleshly lusts / he and his brothers / the Truths with him / who all fought bravely with him against the sin of the dragon. But there follow even further after him: **Jonathan** / **Simon** / **John Hyrcanus** / **Aristobulus** the First / **Alexander Jannaeus** / **Hyrcanus** the King / **Aristobulus** the Second / **Hyrcanus** again / **Antigonus** / **Ananelus** / **Aristobulus Alexander** / **Ananelus** again / **Jesus** / **Simon Boethus** /

# Concerning the Legalistic World.

*Matthias/ Josephus/ Joasar / Eleazar/ Jesus/ Joasar/ Joseph Caiaphas.* <note>These names refer to the historical High Priests of Israel leading up to the time of the New Testament.</note>

These, then, are the hundred Zealots of the Law who were meant to teach within the legalistic soul as saviors and precursors to Christ. They were to tame Israel—meaning the senses and desires—and convert them to the **Lord Jesus** when the time of the Law is fulfilled in the legalistic soul. At that time, Grace must be revealed, and the external "letter" <note>The "letter" refers to the literal, outward observance of religious law as opposed to its spiritual meaning.</note> must be cast aside, so that the Spirit enters the soul like a current and becomes a wellspring of living water. This occurs when **Christ** the **Lord** Himself, as the faithful and true witness, testifies within the soul and brings forth the stream of living water from the source of the heart—from His inner paradisiacal sanctuary—so that this stream may water and bring to life everything that the literal fire-zeal had previously killed under the Law. For until this point, the living Lamb has been strangled within the soul, just as it was strangled under the High Priest Annas <insert>and</insert> Caiaphas at the end of the Kingdom of the Letter. For the Lamb is the new Word of Creation within the Law and within the Spirit. But often, one does not allow the new Word of Creation to bring forth fruit within oneself, but rather strangles it through all kinds of evil thoughts, fleshly lusts, and desires that strive and wage war against the holy laws: just as one can clearly see this condition depicted here under the God-ordained Law of Israel. For nothing happens in the great external world that does not also happen within every individual soul. And this we wish to learn further from the **Tabernacle** <term>Tabernacle: the portable earthly dwelling place of Yahweh used by the Israelites</term> under the God-ordained Law of Israel in the Old Testament.

The **Tabernacle** under the Law is an image of the entire great God-ordained Israel, which until

# Concerning the Legal World.

extends into the New Testament, where the spiritual Israel is revealed.

We see, first, how God Himself made one hundred silver bases upon which it had to be built, so that the boards—representing the outer man—could be firmly set upon them on the four sides and corners. The Tabernacle <note>original: "Stiffts-Hütte," the portable sanctuary used by the Israelites in the wilderness</note> was divided into three parts. The first perimeter all around was made of wood; the second tabernacle consisted of tapestries and was called the Holy Place; the third tabernacle also consisted of tapestries and was called the Most Holy Place. Between the Holy and the Most Holy was a dividing wall, so that they could not come together.

These three parts of the Tabernacle signify the following in a human being: The outer part made of wood signifies the flesh and blood of man. There are 96 silver bases in the outer perimeter, which represent all the large and small bones of the limbs of the body, upon which the tabernacle of the flesh stands and is fastened together. The Holy Tabernacle, made of eleven tapestries, represents the sensory and rational soul-man, where the seven lamps burn ignited in the understanding, and where good incense, prayer, and supplication are offered to God with strong crying and tears. The Most Holy Tabernacle with the ten silk tapestries is the spiritual heart-soul, wherein lies God’s Ark of the Covenant with the living Law, with the Bread from Heaven <note>Manna</note>, and the blossoming rod of Aaron’s zeal; it is there that God’s throne and dwelling place are found, as God says: **I will dwell in them, and walk in them; and I will be their God, and they shall be my people.**

Had God wanted to cast away the outer Jew immediately while he was still sinning, then the Scepter of Judah would surely have been stolen away, and all the masters at his feet in the outer Tabernacle would have perished and would not have seen the Hero <note>original: "Held"; a common mystical term for Christ as the conquering champion of the soul</note> enter into them. But because God had patience, He bore the outer Tabernacle along with the inner, and gave it judges, kings, and high priests at its feet, upon which He built and established it; and He gave them His outer Law and taught them His decrees until the Hero was revealed within them, to whom the nations of the senses and desires thereafter clung, and shall continue to cling.

Thus, this God-legislated world is the world of separation <note>original: "Scheidungs-Welt"; a middle realm where the soul is tested and divided from its impurities</note> which stands in the middle; it is the very world in which we live—namely, Adam with his entire race, as the man of natural life who is placed under the Law. Specifically, he is under the disciplinary Law of God, where he can choose good or evil, life or death, heaven or the prison, God or the Devil. For here man stands upon the stage <note>original: "Schauplatz"; a theatrical metaphor for the world as a place of spiritual performance and observation</note> and is the target at which the shots are aimed. God woos him here; and the Devil, who is cast down to the earth, also woos him with his Beast and false prophets. There

# Concerning the Legalistic World.

Now the World-Adam <note>A term referring to humanity in its fallen, earthly state, still bound by the "old man" of the flesh.</note> limps on both sides: half he lies in death, and half he is in the natural, legalistic life. One moment he wishes to be the Bride of God; the next he wishes to be the Bride of the Devil.

Why do you limp on both sides, you blind Adam? Do you not know which is best: to choose God or the Devil, the light or the darkness, the good or the evil? Have you not read what the Mouth of Truth speaks: **No one can serve two masters?** <note>original: "Niemand kan zweyen Herren dienen?" — a reference to Matthew 6:24.</note> Why then do you do it? But alas, woe! What a great chain of power the Devil has placed upon you through the apostasy from God! His entire power, along with the dark abyss, hangs upon it! And how he pulls you, and holds you so firmly imprisoned! Is there then no Redeemer here who may tear the chain asunder? Yes, certainly! Here is Immanuel <note>Hebrew: "God with us," referring to Christ.</note> with an even greater chain of omnipotence: He can tear the bonds of the Devil to pieces and redeem you from the dungeon of sins! Therefore, surrender yourself to Him with spirit, body, and soul: then you are already helped! For He is like the fire of a goldsmith—as it says in the Prophet <note>A reference to Malachi 3:2-3.</note>—which refines and purifies the silver, and like the soap of the launderers or fullers <note>term: fullers (Walcker) — workers who cleaned and thickened raw cloth.</note> which takes away the filth and impurity. He is the melting and separating fire that can separate the dross from the gold, the good from the evil, the light from the darkness, and actually does separate them in the one who submits to His hand.

But because this world now consists of two such adverse parts—namely, light and darkness—this leads us into a twofold contemplation: to look at each of these two parts, namely the dark part and the light part, individually.

And through these, the seventy heralds were thus raised up again, to preside over the people of Israel in a special way with teaching and life: whom Israel, however, soon rejected, and thus the Esau-like <note>referring to Esau, who traded his birthright</note> and Ishmael-like Judaism was destroyed by the Romans, 70 years after the birth of Christ.

All of this thus takes place in the interior <note>the inner spiritual life of the believer</note>. When the Law of God reigns, then the Spirit of God speaks the fundamental language <note>original: "Grund-Sprache," referring to the pure, original language of creation</note> from the mouths of the seventy princes of the council of Israel to Israel. And when the law of nature, of sin, and of death reigns, then the spirit of Belial <note>a personification of worthlessness or the devil</note> speaks the confused language in the heart, namely the seventy-fold languages from the mouths of the 70 or 72 star-princes of Babel, who lay the foundation for the Tower of Babel in the faculty of reason.

Just as Shem was the end of the confused Babylon with his language, which should be understood according to the foundation of the holy language; and Isaac also broke forth at the end of the 69th in Jacob, to whom he communicated his blessing: So also in the heart, the new man will [emerge] with the most holy fundamental—

The half and full moon in the semi-dark heart, which burns with fire, is the *Hevah* <note>Eve</note> or *Magination* <note>The creative power of the imagination or "Magia"</note> of the God-ordained *Adam*, which is begotten from his light—the sword of the Holy Spirit that proceeds from his mouth. For *Hevah* is one of *Adam’s* spiritual ribs: which ribs are the angelic star-princes who dwell in the light of *Adam* as the living law of God, which he teaches and which is in his heart. This *Hevah*, however, is also made masculine through *Adam*, so that she is just like her husband. For the woman dwells in the man, and the man is her sun who surrounds her: and the woman is *Adam’s* voice and effect, and the law of God just like *Adam*, yet in such a way that the woman remains in the man; otherwise she is a harlot if she departs from her husband, and she loses her true light of life, and finally becomes the serpent and confused *Babylon* <note>A state of spiritual confusion or false doctrine</note>, which adopts a false foundation of teaching and gives birth to it from her mouth.

Just as the *Hevah* who stands in God and *Adam* is God’s Wisdom and *Magination* through which He creates, and who is born in Him as a holy virgin: so it is also constituted with the woman of man. For *Hevah* is *Adam’s* bearer and *Magia* <note>Creative spiritual power</note>, whom he has begotten and born within himself as a virgin: through whom—if she remains in him—he ripens his seed, the Law, and brings it forth from her mouth. # Of the Light-Part

## The Soul's Excellence, Wildness, and Transformation are Considered.

Thank the LORD, O my soul, and you eyes! For you are the light of my life and of my body. You are my life out of the Life that is nothing but Essence and Life. His Spirit has made a beautiful, bright body for my suns. I must compare the solar body to a beautiful crystalline water that is sun-clear and bright within a crystalline glass. Her body is like the pure humanity of a Son of GOD. She is like a mother who has many children on earth and everywhere else. What she brings forth, she nourishes. She gives her children beautiful clothes, which her GOD and most gracious Maker has made for her.

-># On the Soul<-

Esau is a wild apple tree in the springtime. One recognizes him before others by his rough, red, and green garment, because he is a hunter. Although he also parades about in white silk, with all kinds of blossoms and colors—as they are worn at the royal courts—and flourishes greatly upon the earth: yet the fruit that he bears is a sour apple-bite, a wild fruit with a rough skin that is not fit to be eaten, unless it is crushed with a heavy iron pestle <note>original: "Stempel"; refers to a tool used for crushing or stamping</note> and thrown into the winepress until its blood is poured out like streams of water and squeezed out of it, and then filled from one cask into another until its dregs and bitterness remain behind. Thus must Esau’s rough garment and skin, along with his first birthright and blessing, be transformed and given and sold to his brother Jacob, the grafted-in <note>original: "umgezweigten"</note> fat olive tree. So Jacob puts on his brother Esau’s green garment and flourishes very beautifully: green, white, and red. This is my friend, whom I love above all; who is like the beautiful, lowly, and yet fruitful trees that the LORD has branched out. How beautifully my friend blossoms! My friend is very lovely! His scent is like the scent of a field that the LORD has blessed!

Blessed are those trees that stand under the Cross and pressure! For they blossom and are clothed with white silk; and they are followed by the fruits of righteousness, faith, and truthfulness; and they shall be very pleasing to GOD in His lawful Garden of Paradise. They shall blossom forever and bring forth fruit at the same time, better than the springtime trees outside of God’s lawful Garden of Paradise, and shall be more gloriously clothed than those who dwell in royal courts.

# Encouragement to the Praise of GOD.

Praise the LORD, O my soul / you who are the sun of my body and the light of my life! Praised be my GOD / who has given me life / and has Himself become my life; who lets us eat and enjoy the loveliness from the Tree of Life in His lawful Paradise-garden of the Holy Scripture of all Nature! Amen! Hallelujah!

Praise the LORD for this / O my soul!

## Encouragement of the Creature
### to the Praise of GOD.

Praise the LORD, all His creatures! Extol and glorify His great Name / which in truth is holy! The heavens are full of His honor / and the earth of His great wonders! The sun / which is so lovely and bright / proclaims to us His even more glorious and beautiful Majesty / which is glorious above all things. Yes, it shows us / how utterly unchangeable its Maker is. It is so beautiful and bright / that it makes light with its bright rays not only the ground of the earth / which is yet so great and wide / but also the great unfathomable air and depth; by which it wishes to say to us: Look upon me / O you children of men! and all you creatures of God, perceive through me / how beautiful my Maker and Creator must first be / who has made me so beautiful / and has created for me this so wondrously beautiful body, full of light and radiance! For I am the building of His mouth / and through the breath of His lips I have received from Him this soul, full of fire and light. Therefore I have become as beautiful / as a city and son of GOD! I am *Cores* <note>original: "Cores" — a reference to the Persian name for Cyrus, often etymologically linked in early modern mysticism to the sun (Koresh/Khur)</note>, the sun / which from the morning

# of the Name of GOD.

O you children of men! Examine it now / whether it has become better / or whether it has become worse with us? Examine it / O you Popes and fathers of the masses of nations! O you Emperors and Kings! O you teachers and shepherds! You princes and powerful ones! You counts and nobles! You warlords and colonels! You councilmen and merchants! You citizens and servants! You rich and poor! You craftsmen and peasants! Ah, examine it rightly with me / whether it does not still go on this way among us! Let each one according to his station examine it for himself / as dear as your soul is to you! So do it; Come / let us turn back / and humble ourselves before our high **GOD** and Creator! For we / yes, we all have transgressed the holy commandment of life of **GOD** / and have fallen into his disgrace / yes, into his wrath! And all this we know / and yet we have not improved ourselves / and still do not want to improve ourselves!

Ah, examine it / O you dear fellow-creatures! whether all our doing and being does not run contrary to all of nature? If you do not believe it; then come! We will look at it in the light / and especially first take up the **Titles of Honor**; then you will see / how everything is so perverted!

Firstly / the Pope calls himself **GOD’s** Vicar <note>original: "Statthalter" - a deputy or representative acting with the authority of a ruler</note>: and yet mostly all Popes have been destroyers of the City of **GOD** and of the Saints. So a mortal Emperor names and titles himself invincible <note>original: "unüberwindlich" - a standard part of the Imperial title "Semper Augustus"</note>; even though all men are mortal and to be overcome: Fathers / protectors and caregivers of the poor / widows and orphans; even though they are oppressors <note>original: "Schinder" - literally "skinners" or "flayers," implying those who bleed the poor dry through taxes or cruelty</note> of the poor / widows and orphans: their protectors; and yet they destroy them. Also one calls them gracious Lords; even though they [are]

# Rejection

unmerciful and full of the love of money and personal revenge. Furthermore, they are called **Your Serene Highness** <note>original: "Jhro Durchleuchtigkeit"; literally "Your Transparency/Luminosity," a title for high nobility</note>; even though they are carnal, and not Spirit: also **Well-born**; even though they, like all natural men, are born from below according to the flesh! Ah! What more should I say of this? All our doing is perverse, and runs contrary to nature! Every Judas, who possesses but a single purse, wants to be "Jack-at-the-top" <note>A colloquialism for someone seeking the highest social position</note> in the world, so that he might steal something into it!

If our conduct is now contrary to nature; how much more must it be contrary to the Maker of nature, who in the beginning made everything so holy, good, and orderly. Should we not rightly be terrified by such wickedness? But on the contrary! We do not think it so bad; we would gladly make it worse! Is it not so, dear brothers! We surely know that it is wrong? Why then do we do it? We surely know that the **LORD** says:

> I will give my glory to no other, nor my honor to idols! <note>A reference to Isaiah 42:8</note>

Why then do we take honor from one another, which is not due to us mortal creatures? Should godless Popes let themselves be titled **God’s Vicar** <note>original: "Gottes Statthalter"; the deputy or representative of God on Earth</note>; Emperors made of men called "invincible" or "most serene"; wretched mortal men called **Well-born**, **Venerable**, **Right Reverend**, **Your Grace**, **Your Glory**, **Your Royal Majesty**, and so forth, alongside the name of **Jesus Christ**, who is so lowly and humble, yet whose servants they claim to be? Is it not terrifying to hear? Should we not rightly tremble that we rob **God** of His honor? To Him alone belongs this honor, and not to us or to you! He alone is invincible, and not we! **Christ** and the **Holy Spirit** is **God’s Vicar**, and not you!
